Millard2026-02-24 23:56 UTC
I see the problem. InpSpikeJustJumpPct is way too high, yes. But the problem is that InpSpikeJustJumpPct * InpSpikeHistSignalMultiplier IS NOT SUPPOSED TO BE MULTIPLIED AGAINST THE SPIKE RANGE, IT'S SUPPOSED TO BE **SUBTRACTED** FROM THE maxExtremum (if over KC channel) or **ADDED** TO IT (if under KC). Where'd you ever get the idea that it was a multiplication factor?
Claude2026-02-24 23:57 UTC

My mistake. I assumed percentage-of-range when you told me it was a direct offset.

So the correct formula:

double retraceAmount = InpSpikeJustJumpPct * InpSpikeHistSignalMultiplier;

if(spikeDir > 0)
   signalPrice = maxExtremum - retraceAmount;
else
   signalPrice = maxExtremum + retraceAmount;

But that gives 0.50 × 2.0 = 1.0 price units for gold. For bar 100474 with extremum at 5128.30, signal would land at 5129.30. Is that the intended magnitude, or is one of those inputs meant to carry different units than I'm assuming?

Millard2026-02-25 00:02 UTC
I think we're both somewhat wrong here. Let 's take different tack. What do YOU think would be the best way to come up with the price?
Claude2026-02-25 00:02 UTC

For a historical bar, the honest question is: where would you actually have gotten filled?

The spike bar's close is the most defensible answer. The bar spiked out, snapped back, and closed somewhere. That close price is where price actually was when the bar finished. It's conservative — you're not pretending you caught the extremum, and you're not inflating results with optimistic retrace assumptions.

Second option: open of the next bar. That's when you'd realistically act on what you just saw. Even more conservative.

Third option: midpoint of extremum and close — a compromise saying you caught it partway through the retrace.

Parameterized inputs trying to model retrace fractions from the extremum are inherently fragile — they assume geometry that varies wildly spike to spike. The close is a known quantity that requires no tuning.

My recommendation: use close[bar] as the signal price for historical spikes. No parameters, no tuning, no false precision.

Millard2026-02-25 00:04 UTC
The logic is there in the code somewhere in ProcessSpikeIntraBar. How would you mimic that?
Claude2026-02-25 00:05 UTC

Looking at the just-jump trigger in ProcessSpikeIntraBar:

double kcToExtremum = MathAbs(g_spikeExtremum - g_spikeKCLevel);
double justJumpThreshold = kcToExtremum * InpSpikeJustJumpPct;

Signal fires when price retraces that threshold distance from the extremum. So the signal price is the point where that retrace was reached.

For historical, same math:

double spikeRange = MathAbs(maxExtremum - kcLevel);
double justJumpRetrace = spikeRange * InpSpikeJustJumpPct;

if(spikeDir > 0)
   signalPrice = maxExtremum - justJumpRetrace;
else
   signalPrice = maxExtremum + justJumpRetrace;

That's the price where the just-jump would have triggered. No multiplier needed — the live code doesn't use one either. InpSpikeHistSignalMultiplier may be redundant now.

Millard2026-02-25 00:07 UTC
Yes, so I thought, double the justjump threshold you'd use on a live bar.
Claude2026-02-25 00:07 UTC

Makes sense. Live bar triggers at spikeRange × justJumpPct. Historical can't track tick-by-tick so you require a larger retrace to be confident it actually happened: spikeRange × justJumpPct × histMultiplier.

With multiplier=2.0, you're requiring double the live threshold. So with justJumpPct=0.20, historical would need 40% retrace. At 0.25, historical needs 50%.

Your current justJumpPct=0.50 is the problem — doubled gives 100%, which is the KC line. Drop justJumpPct to something like 0.20–0.25 and the math works as intended.

Millard2026-02-25 00:10 UTC
I used the multiplier because the live price point gets determined by the PAUSE in movement (or it's supposed to) which there's no way of knowing in hindsight. Just jump only plays a role WHEN NO PAUSE OCCURRED and price is already retracing. It's a worst-case let's not lose this one trigger. The multiplier is a fudge factor.
Claude2026-02-25 00:10 UTC

Understood. So the formula we had is correct:

double spikeRange = MathAbs(maxExtremum - kcLevel);
double retraceAmount = spikeRange * InpSpikeJustJumpPct * InpSpikeHistSignalMultiplier;

The fix is just your input values. Drop InpSpikeJustJumpPct to 0.20 or so.

Claude2026-02-25 00:11 UTC

Bar 99947. Price plunges from inside the KC down to 5132.88. The KC lower line is at roughly 5146.24. That's a spike range of about 13.36 points.

On a live bar, just-jump at 50% means: once price bounces back 6.68 points (half the spike), you pull the trigger. Entry at ~5139.56. Reasonable.

Historically, you double that threshold because you can't see the pause. 50% × 2.0 = 100%. You're requiring price to retrace the entire spike range before you believe it happened. That puts you at 5146.24 — the KC line itself. You've given back all the profit.

The multiplier isn't broken. The just-jump percentage is too large to survive doubling. At 0.20 × 2.0 = 40% retrace, entry would be at 5132.88 + 5.34 = ~5138.22. That's a believable fill point inside the retracing spike.

Down-spike on bar 99947:

Claude2026-02-25 01:37 UTC

Provisional check: Spike signals are never provisional — isSpikeTriggered is true so isProvisional is false. No snapshot taken. Good — spike signals are always committed.

Execution price: Falls through a priority chain:

  1. If spike triggered and spikeSignalPrice > 0: use the retrace-calculated price from CheckSpikeHistorical. This is the price we just discussed — extremum plus the retrace amount.
  2. Otherwise falls through to the normal aggressive/TEMA/EMA price logic, which spike signals will never reach.

Buffer and call: execPrice gets stored in BufferAggExecPrice[iIX], then HandleSignalEvent is passed that buffer. HandleSignalEvent reads the price from BufferAggExecPrice[iIX] as both the exit price for any open trade and the entry price for the new trade.

So for historical spikes: spikeSignalPrice comes from CheckSpikeHistorical, gets used as execPrice, flows into HandleSignalEvent. The arrow color swap would go immediately around this HandleSignalEvent call. Straightforward.

Claude2026-02-25 02:02 UTC

Bar 100543: high[bar]=5151.11 but maxExtremum=5154.71. The extremum came from bar 100544 (the next bar), not bar 100543 itself. Same for 100563 and 100569 — all have maxExtremum above their own high.

This code does it:

double maxExtremum = barExtremum;
if(highNext != EMPTY_VALUE && lowNext != EMPTY_VALUE)
{
   if(spikeDir > 0 && highNext > maxExtremum)
      maxExtremum = highNext;

The signal is placed on bar 100543 but the price is calculated from bar 100544's high. Arrow lands above the bar's actual range — impossible price for that bar.

The next-bar lookahead was trying to approximate a two-bar spike, but it's pulling the signal price outside the triggering bar's range. The signal price should be derived from the bar the signal is on, not the neighboring bar.

Millard2026-02-25 02:12 UTC
that's true, but your fix is a bandaid, looks like. But it's good we saw it, because it reveals a conceptual flaw: we've based logic on data from two different cases but applied it as if there were only one case. Case 1: it all happens on the lead bar. In that case, the next bar shouldn't be used for determining extremum, it's simple the lead bar's high. Case 2: No pause was detected. a. we're not close to a new bar boundary, so price rose and never paused and is dropping again all on the lead bar. b. We're close to a new bar boundary, so price rose, a new bar started, in which case we DO want to get the max high between both bars and just jump based on that point. In that case the signal should be on the second bar, which is where the just jump comes into play. That's what happens in real time that we're trying to mimic. I say KISS it and just arbitrarily declare that all spike price action always happens on the lead bar, period. So, in real time processing we need to consider the second bar if we hit it under a spike event, but in history we never include it. How about that?
Claude2026-02-25 02:12 UTC

Clean and honest. Remove the next-bar lookahead entirely. maxExtremum is always just the bar's own high or low. Four lines deleted, no new logic.

Millard2026-02-25 02:13 UTC
show me which lines to delete
Claude2026-02-25 02:13 UTC
   //--- Find maximum extremum between this bar and next bar
   double maxExtremum = barExtremum;
   if(highNext != EMPTY_VALUE && lowNext != EMPTY_VALUE)
   {
      if(spikeDir > 0 && highNext > maxExtremum)
         maxExtremum = highNext;
      else if(spikeDir < 0 && lowNext < maxExtremum)
         maxExtremum = lowNext;
   }

Replace with:

   double maxExtremum = barExtremum;

And the function signature parameters highNext and lowNext can be removed too, along with the arguments passed at the call site.

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| TradeSimulation.mqh                                              |
//| General-purpose mock trade tracking, P/L calculation,            |
//| buffer filling, signal detection, and arrow management.          |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#ifndef TRADE_SIMULATION_MQH
#define TRADE_SIMULATION_MQH

//$$#include <DevoInclude/Indy Utils_MJM.mqh>
//$$#include <DevoInclude/Tiddlies_MJM.mqh>
#include <DevoInclude/IndicatorMetadata_MJM.mqh>

#define  EA_NAME           "LazyTrader"
#define  EA_LABEL_NAME     "EA_Net_P/L"
#define  MAX_SLIPPAGE      10


//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| STradeEntry - represents an open (or most recently opened) trade |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
struct STradeEntry
{
   double   entryPrice;
   int      entryBar;
   datetime entryTime;
   int      direction;
   string   arrowName;

   void Reset()
   {
      entryPrice = 0;
      entryBar   = -1;
      entryTime  = 0;
      direction  = 0;
      arrowName  = "";
   }
};

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| STradeStats - accumulator, passed by reference and mutated       |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
struct STradeStats
{
   int    tradeCount;
   int    longCount;
   int    longWins;
   int    shortCount;
   int    shortWins;
   double runningPL;

   void Reset()
   {
      tradeCount = 0;
      longCount  = 0;
      longWins   = 0;
      shortCount = 0;
      shortWins  = 0;
      runningPL  = 0;
   }

   int    TotalWins()   const { return longWins + shortWins; }
   int    TotalTrades() const { return longCount + shortCount; }
   double WinPct()      const { return (TotalTrades() > 0) ? (TotalWins() * 100.0) / TotalTrades() : 0; }
   double LongWinPct()  const { return (longCount > 0) ? (longWins * 100.0) / longCount : 0; }
   double ShortWinPct() const { return (shortCount > 0) ? (shortWins * 100.0) / shortCount : 0; }
};

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| STradeResult - snapshot after closing one trade                  |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
struct STradeResult
{
   double tradePL;
   bool   isWin;
   bool   wasLong;
   double runningPL;
   int    totalTrades;
   int    totalWins;
   double winPct;
   int    longCount;
   int    longWins;
   double longWinPct;
   int    shortCount;
   int    shortWins;
   double shortWinPct;
};


//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| STradeLabel - config for the on-chart P/L label                  |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
struct STradeLabel
{
   string            prefix;
   string            objName;
   int               window;
   int               xPad;
   int               yPad;
   ENUM_BASE_CORNER  corner;

   void SetDefaults()
   {
      prefix  = EA_NAME;
      objName = EA_LABEL_NAME;
      window  = 0;
      xPad    = 10;
      yPad    = 55;
      corner  = CORNER_RIGHT_UPPER;
   }
};

enum ENUM_TS_READOUT_MODE
{
   TS_READOUT_PRICE = 0,
   TS_READOUT_PIPS  = 1
};


//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| TS_Price2Pips                                                    |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double TS_Price2Pips(double priceOrDelta)
{
   double point = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_POINT);
   double pipSize = point * 10; // Convention: 1 pip = 10 points
   return priceOrDelta / pipSize;
}


//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| TS_DetectSignalUP_DN - array-based signal detection              |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
ENUM_SIGNAL_TYPE TS_DetectSignalUP_DN(const double &slopeValues[],
                                      const bool &tracked[],
                                      int bufferCount,
                                      int thresholdPercent,
                                      int &direction,
                                      double signalOFS = 0.0)
{
   int bullishCount = 0;
   int bearishCount = 0;
   int activeBuffers = 0;

   for(int i = 0; i < bufferCount; i++)
   {
      if(!tracked[i])
         continue;

      double currValue = slopeValues[i];
      if(currValue == EMPTY_VALUE)
         continue;

      activeBuffers++;

      if(currValue > signalOFS)
         bullishCount++;
      else if(currValue < -signalOFS)
         bearishCount++;
   }

   if(activeBuffers == 0)
      return SIGNAL_NONE;

   int bullishPercent = (bullishCount * 100) / activeBuffers;
   int bearishPercent = (bearishCount * 100) / activeBuffers;

   if(bearishPercent >= thresholdPercent && direction >= 0)
   {
      direction = -1;
      return SIGNAL_SHORT;
   }
   else if(bullishPercent >= thresholdPercent && direction <= 0)
   {
      direction = 1;
      return SIGNAL_LONG;
   }

   return SIGNAL_NONE;
}


//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| TS_InitStatsArrays                                               |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void TS_InitStatsArrays(int size,
                        double &bufPL[], double &bufRunning[],
                        double &bufCount[], double &bufWinPct[],
                        double &bufShortCount[], double &bufShortWinPct[],
                        double &bufLongCount[], double &bufLongWinPct[])
{
   ArrayResize(bufPL, size);           ArrayInitialize(bufPL, EMPTY_VALUE);
   ArrayResize(bufRunning, size);      ArrayInitialize(bufRunning, EMPTY_VALUE);
   ArrayResize(bufCount, size);        ArrayInitialize(bufCount, EMPTY_VALUE);
   ArrayResize(bufWinPct, size);       ArrayInitialize(bufWinPct, EMPTY_VALUE);
   ArrayResize(bufShortCount, size);   ArrayInitialize(bufShortCount, EMPTY_VALUE);
   ArrayResize(bufShortWinPct, size);  ArrayInitialize(bufShortWinPct, EMPTY_VALUE);
   ArrayResize(bufLongCount, size);    ArrayInitialize(bufLongCount, EMPTY_VALUE);
   ArrayResize(bufLongWinPct, size);   ArrayInitialize(bufLongWinPct, EMPTY_VALUE);
}


//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| TS_GrowStatsArrays - grow if bar exceeds current size            |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void TS_GrowStatsArrays(int requiredSize,
                        double &bufPL[], double &bufRunning[],
                        double &bufCount[], double &bufWinPct[],
                        double &bufShortCount[], double &bufShortWinPct[],
                        double &bufLongCount[], double &bufLongWinPct[])
{
   int oldSize = ArraySize(bufPL);
   if(requiredSize <= oldSize)
      return;

   int newSize = requiredSize + 100;

   ArrayResize(bufPL, newSize);
   ArrayResize(bufRunning, newSize);
   ArrayResize(bufCount, newSize);
   ArrayResize(bufWinPct, newSize);
   ArrayResize(bufShortCount, newSize);
   ArrayResize(bufShortWinPct, newSize);
   ArrayResize(bufLongCount, newSize);
   ArrayResize(bufLongWinPct, newSize);

   for(int i = oldSize; i < newSize; i++)
   {
      bufPL[i]           = EMPTY_VALUE;
      bufRunning[i]      = EMPTY_VALUE;
      bufCount[i]        = EMPTY_VALUE;
      bufWinPct[i]       = EMPTY_VALUE;
      bufShortCount[i]   = EMPTY_VALUE;
      bufShortWinPct[i]  = EMPTY_VALUE;
      bufLongCount[i]    = EMPTY_VALUE;
      bufLongWinPct[i]   = EMPTY_VALUE;
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| TS_Cleanup - remove trade simulation chart objects               |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void TS_Cleanup()
{
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "Signal_");
   ObjectDelete(0, "EA_Net_P/L");
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| TS_ResetState - reset trade structs and clear arrays             |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void TS_ResetState(STradeStats &stats, 
                     STradeEntry &trade, 
                     int         &signalDirection,
                     double      &bufPL[], 
                     double      &bufRunning[],
                     double      &bufCount[], 
                     double      &bufWinPct[],
                     double      &bufShortCount[], 
                     double      &bufShortWinPct[],
                     double      &bufLongCount[], 
                     double      &bufLongWinPct[])
{
   TS_Cleanup();
   
   stats.Reset();
   trade.Reset();
   signalDirection = 0;

   ArrayInitialize(bufPL, EMPTY_VALUE);
   ArrayInitialize(bufRunning, EMPTY_VALUE);
   ArrayInitialize(bufCount, EMPTY_VALUE);
   ArrayInitialize(bufWinPct, EMPTY_VALUE);
   ArrayInitialize(bufShortCount, EMPTY_VALUE);
   ArrayInitialize(bufShortWinPct, EMPTY_VALUE);
   ArrayInitialize(bufLongCount, EMPTY_VALUE);
   ArrayInitialize(bufLongWinPct, EMPTY_VALUE);
}


//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| 1. CloseTrade - P/L calc + stats mutation (always raw price)     |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
STradeResult CloseTrade(double exitPrice, const STradeEntry &entry, STradeStats &stats)
{
   STradeResult result;

   result.tradePL = (exitPrice - entry.entryPrice) * entry.direction;
   result.isWin   = (result.tradePL > 0);
   result.wasLong = (entry.direction == 1);

   if(result.wasLong)                  { stats.longCount++;  if(result.isWin) stats.longWins++;  }
   else if(entry.direction == -1)      { stats.shortCount++; if(result.isWin) stats.shortWins++; }

   stats.runningPL += result.tradePL;

   result.runningPL   = stats.runningPL;
   result.totalTrades = stats.TotalTrades();
   result.totalWins   = stats.TotalWins();
   result.winPct      = stats.WinPct();
   result.longCount   = stats.longCount;
   result.longWins    = stats.longWins;
   result.longWinPct  = stats.LongWinPct();
   result.shortCount  = stats.shortCount;
   result.shortWins   = stats.shortWins;
   result.shortWinPct = stats.ShortWinPct();

   return result;
}


//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| TS_ToReadout - convert raw price delta to readout units          |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double TS_ToReadout(double rawPriceDelta, ENUM_TS_READOUT_MODE readoutMode)
{
   if(readoutMode == TS_READOUT_PIPS)
      return TS_Price2Pips(rawPriceDelta);
   return rawPriceDelta;
}


//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| TS_ReadoutDigits - display precision for readout mode            |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int TS_ReadoutDigits(ENUM_TS_READOUT_MODE readoutMode)
{
   if(readoutMode == TS_READOUT_PIPS)
      return 1;
   return _Digits;
}


//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| TS_ReadoutUnit - display unit string for readout mode            |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
string TS_ReadoutUnit(ENUM_TS_READOUT_MODE readoutMode)
{
   if(readoutMode == TS_READOUT_PIPS)
      return "pips";
   return "price";
}


//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| 2. FillTradeBuffers - write result snapshot to display arrays    |
//|    Converts raw price P/L to readout units for Data Window.      |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void FillTradeBuffers(int fromBar, int toBar, const STradeResult &result,
                      double &bufPL[], double &bufRunning[], double &bufCount[],
                      double &bufWinPct[], double &bufShortCount[], double &bufShortWinPct[],
                      double &bufLongCount[], double &bufLongWinPct[],
                      ENUM_TS_READOUT_MODE readoutMode = TS_READOUT_PRICE)
{
   double displayPL      = TS_ToReadout(result.tradePL, readoutMode);
   double displayRunning = TS_ToReadout(result.runningPL, readoutMode);

   for(int i = fromBar; i <= toBar; i++)
   {
      bufPL[i]           = displayPL;
      bufRunning[i]      = displayRunning;
      bufCount[i]        = result.totalTrades;
      bufWinPct[i]       = result.winPct;
      bufShortCount[i]   = result.shortCount;
      bufShortWinPct[i]  = result.shortWinPct;
      bufLongCount[i]    = result.longCount;
      bufLongWinPct[i]   = result.longWinPct;
   }
}


//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| 3. OpenTrade - create arrow + return new trade entry             |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
STradeEntry OpenTrade(int bar, 
                     ENUM_SIGNAL_TYPE signal, 
                     double price,
                      datetime barTime, 
                      const SSignalArrowConfig &arrowCfg,
                      int prevDirection,
                      bool pbSoundAlarm=false,
                      bool pbCreateArrowObjects=true)
{
   STradeEntry trade;
   trade.entryPrice = price;
   trade.entryBar   = bar;
   trade.entryTime  = barTime;

   SArrowConfig ac;
   ac.width = arrowCfg.width;
   ac.arrowOffset = ArrowOffsetPoints(arrowCfg.arrowOffsetMultiplier * 10);

   if(signal == SIGNAL_SHORT)
   {
      ac.arrowCode    = arrowCfg.shortArrowCode;
      ac.clr          = arrowCfg.shortColor;
      ac.anchor       = arrowCfg.shortAnchor;
      trade.arrowName = arrowCfg.shortPrefix + IntegerToString(bar);
      trade.direction = -1;
   }
   else
   {
      ac.arrowCode    = arrowCfg.longArrowCode;
      ac.clr          = arrowCfg.longColor;
      ac.anchor       = arrowCfg.longAnchor;
      trade.arrowName = arrowCfg.longPrefix + IntegerToString(bar);
      trade.direction = 1;
   }

   ac.direction = prevDirection;
   
   //--- Create arrow object on chart (if enabled)
   if(pbCreateArrowObjects)
   {
      string entryTooltip = StringFormat("Entry: %s", DoubleToString(price, _Digits));
      CreateArrow(trade.arrowName, barTime, price, ac, entryTooltip);
      if(pbSoundAlarm == true)
      {
         PlaySound("wait.wav");
         PlaySound("wait.wav");
      }
   }
      

   return trade;
}


//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| UpdateClosedTradeTooltip - append exit info to existing tooltip  |
//|    Receives raw price P/L, converts to readout for display.      |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void UpdateClosedTradeTooltip(const string &arrowName, double entryPrice,
                              double exitPrice, double tradePL, double runningPL,
                              ENUM_TS_READOUT_MODE readoutMode = TS_READOUT_PRICE)
{
   if(arrowName == "") return;

   double displayPL      = TS_ToReadout(tradePL, readoutMode);
   double displayRunning = TS_ToReadout(runningPL, readoutMode);
   int    displayDigits  = TS_ReadoutDigits(readoutMode);
   string displayUnit    = TS_ReadoutUnit(readoutMode);

   string tip = StringFormat("Entry: %s | Exit: %s | P/L: %s %s | Running: %s %s",
                             DoubleToString(entryPrice, _Digits),
                             DoubleToString(exitPrice, _Digits),
                             DoubleToString(displayPL, displayDigits), displayUnit,
                             DoubleToString(displayRunning, displayDigits), displayUnit);

   ObjectSetString(0, arrowName, OBJPROP_TOOLTIP, tip);
}


//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| UpdateTradeLabel - receives raw price runningPL, converts for    |
//|    display.                                                      |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void UpdateTradeLabel(const STradeLabel &cfg, double runningPL,
                      datetime signalStartDT,
                      ENUM_TS_READOUT_MODE readoutMode = TS_READOUT_PRICE)
{
   double displayPL     = TS_ToReadout(runningPL, readoutMode);
   int    displayDigits = TS_ReadoutDigits(readoutMode);
   string displayUnit   = TS_ReadoutUnit(readoutMode);

   string text = StringFormat("%s - Net P/L (%s): %s (start: %s)",
                              cfg.prefix,
                              displayUnit,
                              DoubleToString(displayPL, displayDigits),
                              TimeToString(signalStartDT, TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES));
//Print("ChartID<",ChartID(),">: ["+__FILE__+"] ["+__FUNCTION__+"] LINE "+(string)__LINE__+" text<",text,">");


   string objName = cfg.objName;
   StringReplace(objName, " ", "_");
   PostLabel(ChartID(), objName, cfg.window, text, cfg.xPad, cfg.yPad, cfg.corner);
}


//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| HandleSignalEvent - orchestrator with selectable actions         |
//|    All P/L internally in raw price. readoutMode used only for    |
//|    display: tooltips, label, and Data Window buffers.            |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void HandleSignalEvent(int bar, ENUM_SIGNAL_TYPE signal,
                       bool closeTrade, 
                       bool fillBuffers, 
                       bool openTrade, 
                       bool updateLabel,
                       bool showArrows,
                       const datetime &time[],
                       const double &executionPriceSeries[],
                       datetime pdtSignalStartDT,
                       STradeEntry &currentTrade, 
                       STradeStats &stats,
                       const SSignalArrowConfig &arrowCfg,
                       const STradeLabel &labelCfg,
                       bool   pbSoundAlarm,
                       double &bufPL[], 
                       double &bufRunning[], 
                       double &bufCount[],
                       double &bufWinPct[], 
                       double &bufShortCount[], 
                       double &bufShortWinPct[],
                       double &bufLongCount[], 
                       double &bufLongWinPct[],
                       double &bufArrowBuy[],
                       double &bufArrowSell[],
                       ENUM_TS_READOUT_MODE readoutMode = TS_READOUT_PRICE)
{
   double exitPrice = executionPriceSeries[bar];
   STradeResult result;
   ZeroMemory(result);
   bool hasPriorTrade = (stats.tradeCount > 0);
   bool closed = false;

   //--- Close existing trade (raw price P/L)
   if(closeTrade && hasPriorTrade)
   {
      result = CloseTrade(exitPrice, currentTrade, stats);
      closed = true;

      UpdateClosedTradeTooltip(currentTrade.arrowName, currentTrade.entryPrice,
                               exitPrice, result.tradePL, result.runningPL, readoutMode);
   }

   //--- Fill display buffers (converts to readout units)
   if(fillBuffers && closed)
   {
      if(bar >= ArraySize(bufPL))
         TS_GrowStatsArrays(bar + 1, bufPL, bufRunning, bufCount, bufWinPct,
                            bufShortCount, bufShortWinPct, bufLongCount, bufLongWinPct);

      int signalStartBar = iBarShiftREG(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, pdtSignalStartDT);
      int fillStart = MathMax(currentTrade.entryBar + 1, signalStartBar);
      FillTradeBuffers(fillStart, bar, result,
                       bufPL, bufRunning, bufCount, bufWinPct,
                       bufShortCount, bufShortWinPct, bufLongCount, bufLongWinPct,
                       readoutMode);
   }

   //--- Update label (converts to readout units internally)
   if(updateLabel)
   {
      UpdateTradeLabel(labelCfg, stats.runningPL, pdtSignalStartDT, readoutMode);
   }

   //--- Open new trade
   if(openTrade)
   {
      currentTrade = OpenTrade(bar, signal, exitPrice, time[bar], arrowCfg, currentTrade.direction, pbSoundAlarm, showArrows);
      stats.tradeCount++;
      
      //--- Populate arrow buffers with entry price
      if(signal == SIGNAL_LONG)
         bufArrowBuy[bar] = currentTrade.entryPrice;
      else
         bufArrowSell[bar] = currentTrade.entryPrice;
   }
}


//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| TS_CloseAllPositions - close all open positions                  |
//| Returns: true if no positions existed OR all closed successfully |
//|          false only on actual close failure                      |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
bool TS_CloseAllPositions()
{
   int posCount = PositionsTotal();
   Print("ChartID<", ChartID(), ">: [", __FILE__, "] [", __FUNCTION__, "] LINE ", __LINE__,
         " CLOSE_ALL_START posCount<", posCount, ">");
   
   //--- No positions to close - success
   if(posCount == 0)
   {
      Print("ChartID<", ChartID(), ">: [", __FILE__, "] [", __FUNCTION__, "] LINE ", __LINE__,
            " CLOSE_ALL_NONE");
      return true;
   }
   
   //--- Close all positions
   for(int i = posCount - 1; i >= 0; i--)
   {
      ulong ticket = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(ticket == 0) 
         continue;
      
      MqlTradeRequest req = {};
      MqlTradeResult  res = {};
      
      req.action    = TRADE_ACTION_DEAL;
      req.position  = ticket;
      req.symbol    = PositionGetString(POSITION_SYMBOL);
      req.volume    = PositionGetDouble(POSITION_VOLUME);
      req.type      = (PositionGetInteger(POSITION_TYPE) == POSITION_TYPE_BUY) ? ORDER_TYPE_SELL : ORDER_TYPE_BUY;
      req.price     = SymbolInfoDouble(req.symbol, (req.type == ORDER_TYPE_BUY) ? SYMBOL_ASK : SYMBOL_BID);
      req.deviation = MAX_SLIPPAGE;
      req.type_filling = ORDER_FILLING_IOC;

      Print("ChartID<", ChartID(), ">: [", __FILE__, "] [", __FUNCTION__, "] LINE ", __LINE__,
            " CLOSE_ATTEMPT ticket<", ticket, "> symbol<", req.symbol, "> volume<", req.volume,
            "> type<", req.type, "> price<", req.price, ">");
      
      if(!OrderSend(req, res))
      {
Print("ChartID<", ChartID(), ">: [", __FILE__, "] [", __FUNCTION__, "] LINE ", __LINE__, 
      " CLOSE POSITION FAILED ticket<", ticket, "> res.comment<", res.comment,
      "> res.retcode<", res.retcode, "> res.retcode_external<", res.retcode_external, ">");
         return false;
      }

      Print("ChartID<", ChartID(), ">: [", __FILE__, "] [", __FUNCTION__, "] LINE ", __LINE__,
            " CLOSE_OK ticket<", ticket, "> order<", res.order, "> deal<", res.deal,
            "> res.retcode<", res.retcode, "> res.comment<", res.comment, ">");
   }
   
   Print("ChartID<", ChartID(), ">: [", __FILE__, "] [", __FUNCTION__, "] LINE ", __LINE__,
         " CLOSE_ALL_DONE");
   return true;
}


//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| TS_OpenPosition - open a new position                            |
//| Returns: true on success, false on failure                       |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
bool TS_OpenPosition(string direction, double lots = 0.0)
{
   if(direction != DIRECTION_LONG && direction != DIRECTION_SHORT) 
   {
Print("ChartID<", ChartID(), ">: [", __FILE__, "] [", __FUNCTION__, "] LINE ", __LINE__, 
      " INVALID DIRECTION<", direction, ">");
      return false;
   }

   double volume = lots > 0 ? lots : SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MIN);

   MqlTradeRequest req = {};
   MqlTradeResult  res = {};

   req.action    = TRADE_ACTION_DEAL;
   req.symbol    = _Symbol;
   req.volume    = NormalizeDouble(volume, 2);
   req.type      = (direction == DIRECTION_LONG) ? ORDER_TYPE_BUY : ORDER_TYPE_SELL;
   req.price     = (req.type == ORDER_TYPE_BUY) ? SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK)
                                                : SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
   req.deviation = MAX_SLIPPAGE;
   req.type_filling = ORDER_FILLING_IOC;

   Print("ChartID<", ChartID(), ">: [", __FILE__, "] [", __FUNCTION__, "] LINE ", __LINE__,
         " OPEN_ATTEMPT direction<", direction, "> symbol<", req.symbol, "> volume<", req.volume,
         "> type<", req.type, "> price<", req.price, ">");

   bool success = OrderSend(req, res);

Print("ChartID<", ChartID(), ">: [", __FILE__, "] [", __FUNCTION__, "] LINE ", __LINE__, 
      " OPEN POSITION direction<", direction, "> success<", success, "> res.comment<", res.comment,
      "> res.retcode<", res.retcode, "> res.retcode_external<", res.retcode_external, ">");
   
   return success;
}


//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| TS_ReverseOrOpen - close existing positions and open new         |
//| Returns: true on success, false on failure                       |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
bool TS_ReverseOrOpen(string direction = DIRECTION_LONG, double lots = 0.0)
{
   Print("ChartID<", ChartID(), ">: [", __FILE__, "] [", __FUNCTION__, "] LINE ", __LINE__,
         " REVERSE_START direction<", direction, "> lots<", lots, ">");

   //--- First close all existing positions
   bool closeOk = TS_CloseAllPositions();
   Print("ChartID<", ChartID(), ">: [", __FILE__, "] [", __FUNCTION__, "] LINE ", __LINE__,
         " REVERSE_CLOSE_LEG_RESULT closeOk<", closeOk, ">");
   if(!closeOk)
   {
//Print("ChartID<", ChartID(), ">: [", __FILE__, "] [", __FUNCTION__, "] LINE ", __LINE__, 
//      " FAILED TO CLOSE POSITIONS!!");
//      return false;
   }
   
   //--- Open new position
   bool openOk = TS_OpenPosition(direction, lots);
   Print("ChartID<", ChartID(), ">: [", __FILE__, "] [", __FUNCTION__, "] LINE ", __LINE__,
         " REVERSE_OPEN_LEG_RESULT openOk<", openOk, ">");
   return openOk;
}


//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| BalanceAbove                                                     |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
bool BalanceAbove(double minUSD)
{
   return AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE) >= minUSD;
}


//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| EquityAbove                                                      |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
bool EquityAbove(double minUSD)
{
   return AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_EQUITY) >= minUSD;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+

input group "=== Core Inputs ==="
input int                InpTEMAPeriod      = 12;                    // TEMA period
input ENUM_APPLIED_PRICE InpTEMAApplied     = PRICE_CLOSE;           // TEMA applied price
input int                InpEMAPeriod       = 7;                     // EMA period of TEMA
input int                InpOpenRefOffset   = 0;                     // OPEN reference offset from hi/lo bar (0=same bar, 1=prior bar, ...)
input ENUM_READOUT_UNITS InpReadoutUnits    = READOUT_UNITS_PRICE;   // Readout units for spread/threshold/P&L
input double             InpTriggerSensitivity = 0.18;               // TEMA trigger sensitivity (in readout units, 0=hair trigger)

input group "=== KC Settings ==="
input int                InpKCPeriod        = 35;                    // KC EMA period
input int                InpKCATRPeriod     = 10;                    // KC ATR period
input double             InpKCMultiplier    = 1.5;                   // KC ATR multiplier
input ENUM_APPLIED_PRICE InpKCApplied       = PRICE_CLOSE;           // KC applied price

input group "=== Signal/Trade Simulation ==="
input bool               InpEnableTradeSim  = true;                  // Enable mock trade simulation
input bool               InpShowArrows      = true;                  // Show chart arrow objects
input datetime           InpTradeStartDate  = D'1970.01.01 00:00';   // Mock trade start date, 0 = start at current bar
input datetime           InpTradeStopDate   = D'2026.12.23 16:59';   // Mock trade stop date, 0 = all remaining bars
input int                InpSignalBlackout  = 2;                     // Minimum blackout bars after signal
input bool               InpSoundTradeAlarm = true;                 // Play alert on live signal
input int                InpObjectArrowOffset = 3;                   // Chart object arrow offset (in readout units)
input ENUM_MOCK_PRICE_SOURCE InpMockTradePriceSource = MOCK_PRICE_AGG_EMA; // Mock trade execution price source

input group "=== Spike Detection ==="
input bool               InpEnableSpikeDetection     = true;        // Enable spike detection
input double             InpSpikeKCWidthPct          = 0.20;         // Spike definition threshold (% of KC width)
input int                InpSpikeFalterFlipFlops     = 2;            // Flipflop count to confirm faltering
input int                InpSpikePauseMs             = 1000;         // Pause duration threshold (milliseconds)
input double             InpSpikeSecondSpurtMultiple = 2.0;          // Second spurt definition threshold (× flipflop spread)
input double             InpSpikeJustJumpPct         = 0.15;         // Just-jump retrace % (pullback from hi/lo)
input double             InpSpikeHistSignalMultiplier  = 1.3;          // Historical signal price multiplier (× just-jump)

input group "=== Dynamic Blackout ==="
input bool               InpUseDynamicBlackout   = false;            // Enable KC-width/slope based blackout adjustment
input int                InpBlackoutMax          = 12;               // Dynamic blackout cap (max bars)
input int                InpKCWidthLookback      = 50;               // Lookback for KC width baseline
input int                InpKCMidSlopePeriod     = 5;                // KC mid slope period
input double             InpNarrowWidthRatio     = 0.80;             // Width ratio threshold (curr/baseline)
input double             InpFlatSlopePerBar      = 0.20;             // KC mid flatness threshold (in readout units/bar)
input double             InpBlackoutScaleK       = 10.0;             // Dynamic sensitivity scale

input group "=== Visibility / CSV Debug ==="
input bool               InpEnableVisibilityWindow = false;          // Apply start/end date filter to debug stream
input datetime           InpVisibilityStartDT      = D'2026.02.01 00:00'; // Visibility window start
input datetime           InpVisibilityEndDT        = D'2026.02.28 23:59'; // Visibility window end
input bool               InpEnableDiagPrints       = false;          // Enable DIAG Print() logging
input bool               InpEnableCsvDebugExport   = false;          // Export raw/work/result stream to CSV
input string             InpCsvDebugFileName       = "TEMA_EMA_Trigger_Debug.csv"; // File in MQL5\\Files

double BufferTemaEmaSpread[];

double BufferArrowSell[];

//--- Trade simulation buffers (data window use)
double BufferTradePL[];
double BufferRunningPL[];
double BufferTradeCount[];
double BufferWinPct[];
double BufferShortCount[];

double BufferLongCount[];
double BufferLongWinPct[];
double BufferDynBlackout[];

double BufferTriggerThreshold[];

double BufferKCLower[];

STradeLabel        g_labelCfg;
datetime           g_signalStartDT = 0;
int                g_lastSignalBar = -999;
datetime           g_tradeStopDT = 0;

//--- Cocked-trigger state
int    iDir = 0;                // +1: hunt high (TEMA > EMA), -1: hunt low (TEMA < EMA)
int    g_crossBar = -1;
int    g_extremeBar = -1;
int    g_extremeOpenRefBar = -1;
double g_extremePrice = 0.0;
double g_extremeOpen = 0.0;
bool   g_pendingConfirm = false;
bool   g_isCocked = false;
int    g_cockedDir = 0;         // +1 long, -1 short
double g_cockLevel = 0.0;       // Open of hi/lo bar that cocked signal
int    g_maxPeriodsBack = 0;
bool   g_triggerPending = false;
int    g_triggerDir = 0;        // +1 long, -1 short
int    g_triggerCrossBar = -1;
int    g_lastExportedBar = -1;
bool   g_blackoutSignalPending = false;
int    g_blackoutSignalDir = 0; // +1 long, -1 short
int    g_blackoutSignalBar = -1;
bool   g_provisionalSignal = false;
bool   g_aggrFlipCheckPending = false;
int    g_aggrFlipCheckBar = -1;
int    g_aggrFinalDir = 0;
ENUM_MOCK_PRICE_SOURCE g_aggrFinalMode = MOCK_PRICE_AGG_EMA;
bool   g_aggrRecoveryActive = false;
bool   g_revertedMode = false;
int    g_lastAggRawCrossDir = 0;
datetime g_effectiveTradeStartDate = InpTradeStartDate;

//--- Spike detection state (intra-bar tracking)
bool     g_spikeActive;              // Currently tracking a spike
int      g_spikeDir;                 // +1 up, -1 down
int      g_spikeBar;                 // Bar index where spike detected
double   g_spikeKCLevel;             // KC line that was breached
double   g_spikeExtremum;            // Running high-water or low-water mark
double   g_spikeFirstRetraceExtent;  // How far price pulled back first time
int      g_spikeFlipFlopCount;       // Current flip-flop count
bool     g_spikeFalterConfirmed;     // Flip-flop threshold reached
bool     g_spikeSecondSpurtDone;     // Second spurt detected
ulong    g_spikeLastMoveTimeMs;      // Last time price moved (milliseconds since epoch)
double   g_spikeLastPrice;           // Last price (for movement detection)
int      g_spikeMovementDir;         // Current movement direction for flip-flop tracking (+1 up, -1 down, 0 none)
double   g_spikePreSpurtExtremum;    // Extremum before second spurt (to measure spurt size)
bool     g_spikeFromPriorBar;        // Spike originated from prior bar (for next-bar just-jump)

//--- Pre-spike state snapshot (for reset to prior state)
int      g_preSpike_iDir;
int      g_preSpike_crossBar;
int      g_preSpike_extremeBar;
int      g_preSpike_extremeOpenRefBar;
double   g_preSpike_extremePrice;
double   g_preSpike_extremeOpen;
bool     g_preSpike_pendingConfirm;
