int      g_preSpike_cockedDir;
double   g_preSpike_cockLevel;
bool     g_preSpike_triggerPending;
int      g_preSpike_triggerDir;
int      g_preSpike_triggerCrossBar;
bool     g_preSpike_blackoutSignalPending;
int      g_preSpike_blackoutSignalDir;
int      g_preSpike_blackoutSignalBar;

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Spike state management functions                                  |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void ResetSpikeState()
{
   g_spikeActive = false;
   g_spikeDir = 0;
   g_spikeBar = -1;
   g_spikeKCLevel = 0.0;
   g_spikeExtremum = 0.0;
   g_spikeFirstRetraceExtent = 0.0;
   g_spikeFlipFlopCount = 0;
   g_spikeFalterConfirmed = false;
   g_spikeSecondSpurtDone = false;
   g_spikeLastMoveTimeMs = 0;
   g_spikeLastPrice = 0.0;
   g_spikeMovementDir = 0;
   g_spikePreSpurtExtremum = 0.0;
   g_spikeFromPriorBar = false;
}

void SnapshotPreSpikeState()
{
   g_preSpike_iDir = iDir;
   g_preSpike_crossBar = g_crossBar;
   g_preSpike_extremeBar = g_extremeBar;
   g_preSpike_extremeOpenRefBar = g_extremeOpenRefBar;
   g_preSpike_extremePrice = g_extremePrice;
   g_preSpike_extremeOpen = g_extremeOpen;
   g_preSpike_pendingConfirm = g_pendingConfirm;
   g_preSpike_isCocked = g_isCocked;
   g_preSpike_cockedDir = g_cockedDir;
   g_preSpike_cockLevel = g_cockLevel;
   g_preSpike_triggerPending = g_triggerPending;
   g_preSpike_triggerDir = g_triggerDir;
   g_preSpike_triggerCrossBar = g_triggerCrossBar;
   g_preSpike_blackoutSignalPending = g_blackoutSignalPending;
   g_preSpike_blackoutSignalDir = g_blackoutSignalDir;
   g_preSpike_blackoutSignalBar = g_blackoutSignalBar;
}

void RestorePreSpikeState()
{
   iDir = g_preSpike_iDir;
   g_crossBar = g_preSpike_crossBar;
   g_extremeBar = g_preSpike_extremeBar;
   g_extremeOpenRefBar = g_preSpike_extremeOpenRefBar;
   g_extremePrice = g_preSpike_extremePrice;
   g_extremeOpen = g_preSpike_extremeOpen;
   g_pendingConfirm = g_preSpike_pendingConfirm;
   g_isCocked = g_preSpike_isCocked;
   g_cockedDir = g_preSpike_cockedDir;
   g_cockLevel = g_preSpike_cockLevel;
   g_triggerPending = g_preSpike_triggerPending;
   g_triggerDir = g_preSpike_triggerDir;
   g_triggerCrossBar = g_preSpike_triggerCrossBar;
   g_blackoutSignalPending = g_preSpike_blackoutSignalPending;
   g_blackoutSignalDir = g_preSpike_blackoutSignalDir;
   g_blackoutSignalBar = g_preSpike_blackoutSignalBar;
   
   ResetSpikeState();
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Readout helpers - convert raw price to display units             |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double PriceDeltaToReadout(const double priceDelta)
{
   if(InpReadoutUnits == READOUT_UNITS_PIPS)
      return TS_Price2Pips(priceDelta);
   return priceDelta;
}

string ReadoutUnitText()
{
   if(InpReadoutUnits == READOUT_UNITS_PIPS)
      return "pips";
   return "price";
}

int ReadoutDigits()
{
   if(InpReadoutUnits == READOUT_UNITS_PIPS)
      return 1;
   return _Digits;
}

ENUM_TS_READOUT_MODE TSReadoutMode()
{
   if(InpReadoutUnits == READOUT_UNITS_PIPS)
      return TS_READOUT_PIPS;
   return TS_READOUT_PRICE;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| ReadoutToPrice - convert user input from readout units to raw    |
//|    price. Called once per OnCalculate to set internal thresholds. |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double ReadoutToPrice(const double readoutValue, const double pipSize)
{
   if(InpReadoutUnits == READOUT_UNITS_PIPS)
      return readoutValue * pipSize;
   return readoutValue;
}

bool IsAggressiveMode()
{
   return (InpMockTradePriceSource >= MOCK_PRICE_AGG_TEMA);
}

double GetMockTradePrice(const int bar, const double &close[])
{
   if(InpMockTradePriceSource == MOCK_PRICE_TEMA && BufferTEMA[bar] != EMPTY_VALUE)
      return BufferTEMA[bar];
   if(InpMockTradePriceSource == MOCK_PRICE_EMA_TEMA && BufferEMAofTEMA[bar] != EMPTY_VALUE)
      return BufferEMAofTEMA[bar];
   return close[bar];
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void UpdateOpenTradeLiveState(const int bar, const double liveExitPrice)
{
   if(g_currentTrade.direction == 0 || g_currentTrade.entryBar < 0)
      return;

   if(bar >= ArraySize(BufferTradePL))
      TS_GrowStatsArrays(bar + 1, BufferTradePL, BufferRunningPL, BufferTradeCount, BufferWinPct,
                         BufferShortCount, BufferShortWinPct, BufferLongCount, BufferLongWinPct);

   //--- Compute in raw price
   double livePL = (liveExitPrice - g_currentTrade.entryPrice) * g_currentTrade.direction;
   double liveRunning = g_tradeStats.runningPL + livePL;

   //--- Store display values in Data Window buffers
   BufferTradePL[bar]    = PriceDeltaToReadout(livePL);
   BufferRunningPL[bar]  = PriceDeltaToReadout(liveRunning);
   BufferTradeCount[bar] = g_tradeStats.TotalTrades();
   BufferWinPct[bar]     = g_tradeStats.WinPct();
   BufferShortCount[bar] = g_tradeStats.shortCount;
   BufferShortWinPct[bar]= g_tradeStats.ShortWinPct();
   BufferLongCount[bar]  = g_tradeStats.longCount;
   BufferLongWinPct[bar] = g_tradeStats.LongWinPct();

   if(g_currentTrade.arrowName != "" && ObjectFind(0, g_currentTrade.arrowName) >= 0)
   {
      int displayDigits   = ReadoutDigits();
      string readoutUnit  = ReadoutUnitText();
      string tip = StringFormat("Entry: %s | Exit: %s | P/L: %s %s | Running: %s %s",
                                DoubleToString(g_currentTrade.entryPrice, _Digits),
                                DoubleToString(liveExitPrice, _Digits),
                                DoubleToString(BufferTradePL[bar], displayDigits), readoutUnit,
                                DoubleToString(BufferRunningPL[bar], displayDigits), readoutUnit);
      ObjectSetString(0, g_currentTrade.arrowName, OBJPROP_TOOLTIP, tip);
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int T2ESign(double v, double eps)
{
   if(v > eps)  return 1;
   if(v < -eps) return -1;
   return 0;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int KCOuterState(const double lineValue,
                 const int lineToMidState,
                 const double kcUpper,
                 const double kcLower,
                 const double eps)
{
   if(lineToMidState == 0)
      return 0;
   if(lineValue == EMPTY_VALUE || kcUpper == EMPTY_VALUE || kcLower == EMPTY_VALUE)
      return 0;

   double selectedOuter = (lineToMidState > 0) ? kcUpper : kcLower;
   return T2ESign((lineValue - selectedOuter) * lineToMidState, eps);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int EffectiveAggressiveBlackout(const int bar)
{
   double blackout = InpSignalBlackout;
   if(bar >= 0 && bar < ArraySize(BufferDynBlackout) && BufferDynBlackout[bar] != EMPTY_VALUE)
      blackout = BufferDynBlackout[bar];

   blackout = blackout - 1.0;
   if(blackout < 0.0)
      blackout = 0.0;
   return (int)MathRound(blackout);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void ArmAggressiveFlipCheck(const int finalizedBar,
                            const int finalizedDir,
                            const ENUM_MOCK_PRICE_SOURCE finalizedMode)
{
   if(finalizedBar < 0 || finalizedDir == 0)
      return;

   g_aggrFlipCheckPending = true;
   g_aggrFlipCheckBar = finalizedBar + EffectiveAggressiveBlackout(finalizedBar);
   g_aggrFinalDir = finalizedDir;
   g_aggrFinalMode = finalizedMode;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
bool IsWrongSideAggressiveTarget(const int bar,
                                 const double &close[],
                                 const double triggerSensPrice,
                                 const int finalizedDir,
                                 const ENUM_MOCK_PRICE_SOURCE finalizedMode)
{
   if(finalizedDir == 0 || bar < 0)
      return false;

   double base = EMPTY_VALUE;
   if(finalizedMode == MOCK_PRICE_AGG_TEMA)
      base = BufferTEMA[bar];
   else if(finalizedMode == MOCK_PRICE_AGG_EMA)
      base = BufferEMAofTEMA[bar];

   if(base == EMPTY_VALUE)
      return false;

   double target = base + finalizedDir * triggerSensPrice;
   if(finalizedDir > 0)
      return (close[bar] < target);
   return (close[bar] > target);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
string SignalToText(ENUM_SIGNAL_TYPE signal)
{
   if(signal == SIGNAL_LONG)  return "LONG";
   if(signal == SIGNAL_SHORT) return "SHORT";
   return "NONE";
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
bool IsInVisibilityWindow(datetime dt)
{
   if(!InpEnableVisibilityWindow)
      return true;

   return (dt >= InpVisibilityStartDT && dt <= InpVisibilityEndDT);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void InitCsvDebugFile()
{
   if(!InpEnableCsvDebugExport)
      return;

   FileDelete(InpCsvDebugFileName);
   int h = FileOpen(InpCsvDebugFileName, FILE_WRITE|FILE_CSV|FILE_ANSI, ',');
   if(h == INVALID_HANDLE)
   {
      Print("ChartID<",ChartID(),">: [",__FILE__,"] [",__FUNCTION__,"] LINE ",__LINE__,
            " CSV init failed. file<",InpCsvDebugFileName,"> err=",GetLastError());
      return;
   }

   string readoutUnit = ReadoutUnitText();
   FileWrite(h,
             "time","bar","open","high","low","close",
             "tema","ema_tema",
             "kc_upper","kc_mid","kc_lower",
             "kc_width_"+readoutUnit,"kc_width_ratio","kc_mid_slope_"+readoutUnit+"_per_bar",
             "active_blackout",
             "direction","cross_up","cross_dn","is_cross",
             "extreme_bar","extreme_open_ref_bar","extreme_open","extreme_price",
             "pending_confirm","is_cocked","cocked_dir","cock_level",
             "trigger_pending","trigger_dir","trigger_cross_bar",
             "spike_active","spike_dir","spike_falter","spike_second_spurt",
             "signal","arrow_buy","arrow_sell",
             "trade_count","running_pl_"+readoutUnit);
   FileClose(h);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void AppendCsvDebugRow(const datetime dt, 
                        const int bar,
                        const double opn, 
                        const double hi, 
                        const double lo, 
                        const double cls,
                        const double tema, 
                        const double emaTema,
                        const double kcUpper, 
                        const double kcMid, 
                        const double kcLower,
                        const double kcWidthReadout, 
                        const double kcWidthRatio, 
                        const double kcMidSlopeReadout,
                        const double activeBlackout,
                        const bool crossUp, 
                        const bool crossDn, 
                        const bool isCross,
                        const ENUM_SIGNAL_TYPE signal)
{
   if(!InpEnableCsvDebugExport)
      return;
   if(!IsInVisibilityWindow(dt))
      return;

   int h = FileOpen(InpCsvDebugFileName, FILE_READ|FILE_WRITE|FILE_CSV|FILE_ANSI|FILE_SHARE_READ, ',');
   if(h == INVALID_HANDLE)
      return;

   FileSeek(h, 0, SEEK_END);
   FileWrite(h,
             TimeToString(dt, TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES),
             bar,
             opn, hi, lo, cls,
             tema, emaTema,
             kcUpper, kcMid, kcLower,
             kcWidthReadout, kcWidthRatio, kcMidSlopeReadout,
             activeBlackout,
             iDir, (int)crossUp, (int)crossDn, (int)isCross,
             g_extremeBar, g_extremeOpenRefBar, g_extremeOpen, g_extremePrice,
             (int)g_pendingConfirm, (int)g_isCocked, g_cockedDir, g_cockLevel,
             (int)g_triggerPending, g_triggerDir, g_triggerCrossBar,
             (int)g_spikeActive, g_spikeDir, (int)g_spikeFalterConfirmed, (int)g_spikeSecondSpurtDone,
             SignalToText(signal), BufferArrowBuy[bar], BufferArrowSell[bar],
             g_tradeStats.tradeCount, PriceDeltaToReadout(g_tradeStats.runningPL));
   FileClose(h);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void UpdateExtremeOpenReference(int extremeBar, const double &open[])
{
   g_extremeOpenRefBar = MathMax(0, extremeBar - InpOpenRefOffset);
   g_extremeOpen = open[g_extremeOpenRefBar];
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void ResetCycleForDirection(int iDirection, int bar, const double &open[], const double &high[], const double &low[],
                            bool preserveTriggerState = false)
{
   iDir = iDirection;
   g_crossBar = bar;
   g_extremeBar = bar;
   UpdateExtremeOpenReference(g_extremeBar, open);
   g_pendingConfirm = true;

   if(iDirection > 0)
      g_extremePrice = high[bar];
   else
      g_extremePrice = low[bar];

   g_isCocked = false;
   g_cockedDir = 0;
   g_cockLevel = 0.0;
   if(!preserveTriggerState)
   {
      g_triggerPending = false;
      g_triggerDir = 0;
      g_triggerCrossBar = -1;
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void ResetAllStateAndBuffers()
{
   g_tradeStats.Reset();
   g_currentTrade.Reset();
   g_snapshotStats.Reset();
   g_snapshotTrade.Reset();
   g_signalStartDT = 0;
   g_lastSignalBar = -999;

   iDir = 0;
   g_crossBar = -1;
   g_extremeBar = -1;
   g_extremeOpenRefBar = -1;
   g_extremePrice = 0.0;
   g_extremeOpen = 0.0;
   g_pendingConfirm = false;
   g_isCocked = false;
   g_cockedDir = 0;
   g_cockLevel = 0.0;
   g_triggerPending = false;
   g_triggerDir = 0;
   g_triggerCrossBar = -1;
   g_blackoutSignalPending = false;
   g_blackoutSignalDir = 0;
   g_blackoutSignalBar = -1;
   g_provisionalSignal = false;
   g_aggrFlipCheckPending = false;
   g_aggrFlipCheckBar = -1;
   g_aggrFinalDir = 0;
   g_aggrFinalMode = MOCK_PRICE_AGG_EMA;
   g_aggrRecoveryActive = false;
   g_revertedMode = false;
   g_lastAggRawCrossDir = 0;
   
   //--- Reset spike state
   ResetSpikeState();
   g_preSpike_iDir = 0;
   g_preSpike_crossBar = -1;
   g_preSpike_extremeBar = -1;
   g_preSpike_extremeOpenRefBar = -1;
   g_preSpike_extremePrice = 0.0;
   g_preSpike_extremeOpen = 0.0;
   g_preSpike_pendingConfirm = false;
   g_preSpike_isCocked = false;
   g_preSpike_cockedDir = 0;
   g_preSpike_cockLevel = 0.0;
   g_preSpike_triggerPending = false;
   g_preSpike_triggerDir = 0;
   g_preSpike_triggerCrossBar = -1;
   g_preSpike_blackoutSignalPending = false;
   g_preSpike_blackoutSignalDir = 0;
   g_preSpike_blackoutSignalBar = -1;
   
   ArrayInitialize(BufferArrowBuy, EMPTY_VALUE);
   ArrayInitialize(BufferArrowSell, EMPTY_VALUE);
   ArrayInitialize(BufferTradePL, EMPTY_VALUE);
   ArrayInitialize(BufferRunningPL, EMPTY_VALUE);
   ArrayInitialize(BufferTradeCount, EMPTY_VALUE);
   ArrayInitialize(BufferWinPct, EMPTY_VALUE);
   ArrayInitialize(BufferShortCount, EMPTY_VALUE);
   ArrayInitialize(BufferShortWinPct, EMPTY_VALUE);
   ArrayInitialize(BufferLongCount, EMPTY_VALUE);
   ArrayInitialize(BufferLongWinPct, EMPTY_VALUE);
   ArrayInitialize(BufferDynBlackout, EMPTY_VALUE);
   ArrayInitialize(BufferKCWidthRatio, EMPTY_VALUE);
   ArrayInitialize(BufferTriggerThreshold, EMPTY_VALUE);
   ArrayInitialize(BufferTemaEmaSpread, EMPTY_VALUE);

   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, SIGNAL_PREFIX+"_");
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void RevertProvisionalSignal(const int bar)
{
   ObjectDelete(0, g_currentTrade.arrowName);
   BufferArrowBuy[bar] = EMPTY_VALUE;
   BufferArrowSell[bar] = EMPTY_VALUE;
   g_currentTrade = g_snapshotTrade;
   g_tradeStats = g_snapshotStats;
   g_provisionalSignal = false;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Spike Detection: Check for KC breach + sufficient extension       |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
bool CheckSpikeCondition(const int bar,
                         const double barHigh,
                         const double barLow,
                         const double barClose,
                         const double kcUpper,
                         const double kcLower)
{
   if(!InpEnableSpikeDetection)
      return false;
   if(kcUpper == EMPTY_VALUE || kcLower == EMPTY_VALUE)
      return false;
   if(g_spikeActive)
      return false;  // Already tracking a spike
      
   //--- Calculate threshold as percentage of KC width
   double kcWidth = kcUpper - kcLower;
   if(kcWidth <= 0)
      return false;
   double dSpikeExtentMIN = InpSpikeKCWidthPct * kcWidth;
   
   //--- Calculate extension beyond KC (not bar range)
   double dOutsideKC_UP = (barHigh > kcUpper) ? (barHigh - kcUpper) : 0.0;
   double dOutsideKC_DN = (barLow < kcLower) ? (kcLower - barLow) : 0.0;
   
   bool breachUp = (dOutsideKC_UP > 0);
   bool breachDn = (dOutsideKC_DN > 0);
   
   if(!breachUp && !breachDn)
      return false;
      
   bool qualifiedUp = (dOutsideKC_UP >= dSpikeExtentMIN);
   bool qualifiedDn = (dOutsideKC_DN >= dSpikeExtentMIN);
   
   if(!qualifiedUp && !qualifiedDn)
      return false;
      
   //--- Determine spike direction (if both qualified, use larger extension)
   int spikeDir = 0;
   double kcLevel = 0.0;
   
   if(qualifiedUp && qualifiedDn)
   {
      if(dOutsideKC_UP >= dOutsideKC_DN)
      {
         spikeDir = 1;
         kcLevel = kcUpper;
      }
      else
      {
         spikeDir = -1;
         kcLevel = kcLower;
      }
   }
   else if(qualifiedUp)
   {
      spikeDir = 1;
      kcLevel = kcUpper;
   }
   else
   {
      spikeDir = -1;
      kcLevel = kcLower;
   }
   
   //--- Spike must align with current hunting direction (or no direction yet)
   if(iDir != 0 && spikeDir != iDir)
   {
      if(InpEnableDiagPrints)
         Print("ChartID<",ChartID(),">: [",__FILE__,"] [",__FUNCTION__,"] LINE ",__LINE__,
               " SPIKE_IGNORED_OPPOSITE_DIR spikeDir<",spikeDir,"> iDir<",iDir,">");
      return false;
   }
   
   //--- Snapshot pre-spike state before modifying anything
   SnapshotPreSpikeState();
   
   //--- Initialize spike tracking
   g_spikeActive = true;
   g_spikeDir = spikeDir;
   g_spikeBar = bar;
   g_spikeKCLevel = kcLevel;
   g_spikeExtremum = (spikeDir > 0) ? barHigh : barLow;
   g_spikeFirstRetraceExtent = 0.0;
   g_spikeFlipFlopCount = 0;
   g_spikeFalterConfirmed = false;
   g_spikeSecondSpurtDone = false;
   g_spikeLastMoveTimeMs = GetTickCount64();
   g_spikeLastPrice = barClose;
   g_spikeMovementDir = 0;
   g_spikePreSpurtExtremum = g_spikeExtremum;
   g_spikeFromPriorBar = false;
   
   if(InpEnableDiagPrints)
      Print("ChartID<",ChartID(),">: [",__FILE__,"] [",__FUNCTION__,"] LINE ",__LINE__,
            " SPIKE_DETECTED bar<",bar,"> dir<",spikeDir,"> kcLevel<",kcLevel,
            "> extremum<",g_spikeExtremum,"> extension<",(spikeDir > 0 ? dOutsideKC_UP : dOutsideKC_DN),
            "> threshold<",dSpikeExtentMIN,"> kcWidth<",kcWidth,">");
   
   return true;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Spike Intra-bar Processing: Track faltering, second spurt, pause  |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
ENUM_SIGNAL_TYPE ProcessSpikeIntraBar(const int bar,
                                       const double currentPrice,
                                       const double barHigh,
                                       const double barLow,
                                       const datetime barTime)
{
   if(!g_spikeActive)
      return SIGNAL_NONE;
      
   ulong currentTimeMs = GetTickCount64();
   ENUM_SIGNAL_TYPE spikeSignal = SIGNAL_NONE;
   
   //--- Update extremum if price extends further in spike direction
   bool newExtremum = false;
   if(g_spikeDir > 0 && barHigh > g_spikeExtremum)
   {
      g_spikeExtremum = barHigh;
      newExtremum = true;
   }
   else if(g_spikeDir < 0 && barLow < g_spikeExtremum)
   {
      g_spikeExtremum = barLow;
      newExtremum = true;
   }
   
   //--- Track price movement direction for flip-flop counting
   int priceDir = 0;
   if(currentPrice > g_spikeLastPrice)
      priceDir = 1;
   else if(currentPrice < g_spikeLastPrice)
      priceDir = -1;
      
   //--- Detect flip-flops (direction reversal then reversal back)
   if(priceDir != 0 && g_spikeMovementDir != 0 && priceDir != g_spikeMovementDir)
   {
      //--- Direction changed - this is half a flip-flop
      //--- Count full flip-flop when returning to original direction
      if((g_spikeDir > 0 && priceDir > 0) || (g_spikeDir < 0 && priceDir < 0))
      {
         //--- Moving back toward spike direction after moving away = flip-flop complete
         g_spikeFlipFlopCount++;
         
         if(InpEnableDiagPrints)
            Print("ChartID<",ChartID(),">: [",__FILE__,"] [",__FUNCTION__,"] LINE ",__LINE__,
                  " SPIKE_FLIPFLOP count<",g_spikeFlipFlopCount,"> bar<",bar,">");
      }
      
      //--- Track first retrace extent (for second spurt threshold)
      if(g_spikeFirstRetraceExtent == 0.0 && !g_spikeFalterConfirmed)
      {
         if(g_spikeDir > 0)
            g_spikeFirstRetraceExtent = g_spikeExtremum - barLow;
         else
            g_spikeFirstRetraceExtent = barHigh - g_spikeExtremum;
      }
   }
   
   //--- Update movement tracking
   if(priceDir != 0)
   {
      g_spikeMovementDir = priceDir;
      g_spikeLastMoveTimeMs = currentTimeMs;
   }
   g_spikeLastPrice = currentPrice;
   
   //--- Check for faltering confirmation
   if(!g_spikeFalterConfirmed && g_spikeFlipFlopCount >= InpSpikeFalterFlipFlops)
   {
      g_spikeFalterConfirmed = true;
      g_spikePreSpurtExtremum = g_spikeExtremum;
      
      if(InpEnableDiagPrints)
         Print("ChartID<",ChartID(),">: [",__FILE__,"] [",__FUNCTION__,"] LINE ",__LINE__,
               " SPIKE_FALTER_CONFIRMED bar<",bar,"> preSpurtExtremum<",g_spikePreSpurtExtremum,">");
   }
   
   //--- Check for second spurt (after faltering confirmed)
   if(g_spikeFalterConfirmed && !g_spikeSecondSpurtDone)
   {
      double spurtThreshold = g_spikeFirstRetraceExtent * InpSpikeSecondSpurtMultiple;
      double spurtDistance = 0.0;
      
      if(g_spikeDir > 0)
         spurtDistance = g_spikeExtremum - g_spikePreSpurtExtremum;
      else
         spurtDistance = g_spikePreSpurtExtremum - g_spikeExtremum;
         
      if(spurtDistance >= spurtThreshold && spurtThreshold > 0)
      {
         g_spikeSecondSpurtDone = true;
         g_spikeLastMoveTimeMs = currentTimeMs;  // Reset pause timer
         
         if(InpEnableDiagPrints)
            Print("ChartID<",ChartID(),">: [",__FILE__,"] [",__FUNCTION__,"] LINE ",__LINE__,
                  " SPIKE_SECOND_SPURT bar<",bar,"> spurtDistance<",spurtDistance,
                  "> threshold<",spurtThreshold,">");
      }
   }
   
   //--- Check for PAUSE trigger (after second spurt)
   if(g_spikeSecondSpurtDone)
   {
      ulong elapsedMs = currentTimeMs - g_spikeLastMoveTimeMs;
      
      if(elapsedMs >= (ulong)InpSpikePauseMs)
      {
         //--- PAUSE DETECTED - TRIGGER SIGNAL OPPOSITE TO SPIKE
         spikeSignal = (g_spikeDir > 0) ? SIGNAL_SHORT : SIGNAL_LONG;
         
         if(InpEnableDiagPrints)
            Print("ChartID<",ChartID(),">: [",__FILE__,"] [",__FUNCTION__,"] LINE ",__LINE__,
                  " SPIKE_PAUSE_TRIGGER bar<",bar,"> elapsedMs<",elapsedMs,
                  "> signal<",SignalToText(spikeSignal),">");
                  
         ResetSpikeState();
         return spikeSignal;
      }
   }
   
   //--- Check for JUST-JUMP trigger (immediate retrace without pause)
   double kcToExtremum = MathAbs(g_spikeExtremum - g_spikeKCLevel);
   double justJumpThreshold = kcToExtremum * InpSpikeJustJumpPct;
   double currentRetrace = 0.0;
   
   if(g_spikeDir > 0)
      currentRetrace = g_spikeExtremum - currentPrice;
   else
      currentRetrace = currentPrice - g_spikeExtremum;
      
   if(currentRetrace >= justJumpThreshold && justJumpThreshold > 0)
   {
      //--- JUST-JUMP - Price retraced enough, trigger immediately
      spikeSignal = (g_spikeDir > 0) ? SIGNAL_SHORT : SIGNAL_LONG;
      
      if(InpEnableDiagPrints)
         Print("ChartID<",ChartID(),">: [",__FILE__,"] [",__FUNCTION__,"] LINE ",__LINE__,
               " SPIKE_JUSTJUMP_TRIGGER bar<",bar,"> retrace<",currentRetrace,
               "> threshold<",justJumpThreshold,"> signal<",SignalToText(spikeSignal),">");
               
      ResetSpikeState();
      return spikeSignal;
   }
   
   return SIGNAL_NONE;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Check for spike reset condition (KC cross-back)                   |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
bool CheckSpikeReset(const int bar,
                     const double currentPrice,
                     const double kcUpper,
                     const double kcLower)
{
   if(!g_spikeActive)
      return false;
      
   //--- Check if price crossed back through the same KC line in opposite direction
   bool resetCondition = false;
   
   if(g_spikeDir > 0)
   {
      //--- Up spike: reset if price drops below KC upper (crosses back down)
      if(currentPrice < g_spikeKCLevel)
         resetCondition = true;
   }
   else
   {
      //--- Down spike: reset if price rises above KC lower (crosses back up)
      if(currentPrice > g_spikeKCLevel)
         resetCondition = true;
   }
   
   if(resetCondition)
   {
      if(InpEnableDiagPrints)
         Print("ChartID<",ChartID(),">: [",__FILE__,"] [",__FUNCTION__,"] LINE ",__LINE__,
               " SPIKE_RESET bar<",bar,"> restoring pre-spike state");
               
      RestorePreSpikeState();
      return true;
   }
   
   return false;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Historical Spike Detection for closed bars                        |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
ENUM_SIGNAL_TYPE CheckSpikeHistorical(const int bar,
                                       const double &high[],
                                       const double &low[],
                                       const double kcUpper,
                                       const double kcLower,
//                                       const double highNext,
//                                       const double lowNext,
                                       double &signalPrice)
{
   signalPrice = 0.0;
   
   if(!InpEnableSpikeDetection)
      return SIGNAL_NONE;
   if(kcUpper == EMPTY_VALUE || kcLower == EMPTY_VALUE)
      return SIGNAL_NONE;
      
   //--- Calculate threshold as percentage of KC width
   double kcWidth = kcUpper - kcLower;
   if(kcWidth <= 0)
      return SIGNAL_NONE;
   double dSpikeExtentMIN = InpSpikeKCWidthPct * kcWidth;
   
   //--- Calculate extension beyond KC for this bar
   double dOutsideKC_UP = (high[bar] > kcUpper) ? (high[bar] - kcUpper) : 0.0;
   double dOutsideKC_DN = (low[bar] < kcLower) ? (kcLower - low[bar]) : 0.0;
//   double dInsideKC_UP = (kcUpper > low[bar]) ? (kcUpper - low[bar]) : 0.0;
//   double dInsideKC_DN = (kcLower < high[bar]) ? (high[bar] - kcLower) : 0.0;
//
   bool qualifiedUp = (dOutsideKC_UP >= dSpikeExtentMIN && low[bar] < kcUpper);
   bool qualifiedDn = (dOutsideKC_DN >= dSpikeExtentMIN && high[bar] > kcLower);
   
   if(!qualifiedUp && !qualifiedDn)
      return SIGNAL_NONE;
      
   //--- Determine spike direction (if both qualified, use larger extension)
   int spikeDir = 0;
   double kcLevel = 0.0;
   double barExtremum = 0.0;
   
   if(qualifiedUp && qualifiedDn)
   {
      if(dOutsideKC_UP >= dOutsideKC_DN)
      {
         spikeDir = 1;
         kcLevel = kcUpper;
         barExtremum = high[bar];
      }
      else
      {
         spikeDir = -1;
         kcLevel = kcLower;
         barExtremum = low[bar];
      }
   }
   else if(qualifiedUp)
   {
      spikeDir = 1;
      kcLevel = kcUpper;
      barExtremum = high[bar];
   }
   else
   {
      spikeDir = -1;
      kcLevel = kcLower;
      barExtremum = low[bar];
   }
   
   //--- Spike must align with current hunting direction (or no direction yet)
   if(iDir != 0 && spikeDir != iDir)
   {
      if(InpEnableDiagPrints)
         Print("ChartID<",ChartID(),">: [",__FILE__,"] [",__FUNCTION__,"] LINE ",__LINE__,
               " SPIKE_HISTORICAL_IGNORED_OPPOSITE_DIR bar<",bar,"> spikeDir<",spikeDir,"> iDir<",iDir,">");
      return SIGNAL_NONE;
   }
   
   //--- Find maximum extremum between this bar and next bar
   double maxExtremum = barExtremum;
//   if(highNext != EMPTY_VALUE && lowNext != EMPTY_VALUE)
//   {
//      if(spikeDir > 0 && highNext > maxExtremum)
//         maxExtremum = highNext;
//      else if(spikeDir < 0 && lowNext < maxExtremum)
//         maxExtremum = lowNext;
//   }
//   
   //--- Calculate signal price using historical multiplier and just-jump percentage
   double spikeRange = MathAbs(maxExtremum - kcLevel);
   double retraceAmount = spikeRange * InpSpikeJustJumpPct * InpSpikeHistSignalMultiplier;
//   double retraceAmount = MathAbs(maxExtremum - kcLevel) - (InpSpikeHistSignalMultiplier * InpSpikeJustJumpPct);
   
   if(spikeDir > 0)
      signalPrice = maxExtremum - retraceAmount;
   else
      signalPrice = maxExtremum + retraceAmount;
   
   //--- Signal is opposite to spike direction
   ENUM_SIGNAL_TYPE signal = (spikeDir > 0) ? SIGNAL_SHORT : SIGNAL_LONG;
   
   if(InpEnableDiagPrints)
      Print("ChartID<",ChartID(),">: [",__FILE__,"] [",__FUNCTION__,"] LINE ",__LINE__,
            " SPIKE_HISTORICAL_DETECTED bar<",bar,"> spikeDir<",spikeDir,
            "> maxExtremum<",maxExtremum,"> signalPrice<",signalPrice,
            "> dSpikeExtentMIN<",dSpikeExtentMIN,"> dOutsideKC_UP<",dOutsideKC_UP,"> dOutsideKC_DN<",dOutsideKC_DN,
            "> high[bar]<",high[bar],"> low[bar]<",low[bar],
            "> signal<",SignalToText(signal),">");

   return signal;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int OnInit()
{
   if(InpTEMAPeriod < 1 || InpEMAPeriod < 1 || InpOpenRefOffset < 0 ||
      InpTriggerSensitivity < 0.0 || InpObjectArrowOffset < 0 ||
      InpKCPeriod < 1 || InpKCATRPeriod < 1 || InpKCMultiplier <= 0.0 ||
      InpSignalBlackout < 0 || InpBlackoutMax < InpSignalBlackout ||
      InpKCWidthLookback < 1 || InpKCMidSlopePeriod < 1 ||
      InpNarrowWidthRatio <= 0.0 || InpFlatSlopePerBar < 0.0 || InpBlackoutScaleK < 0.0 ||
      (InpEnableVisibilityWindow && InpVisibilityEndDT < InpVisibilityStartDT) ||
      InpSpikeKCWidthPct <= 0.0 || InpSpikeKCWidthPct > 1.0 ||
      InpSpikeFalterFlipFlops < 1 || InpSpikePauseMs < 0 ||
      InpSpikeSecondSpurtMultiple <= 0.0 || 
      InpSpikeJustJumpPct <= 0.0 || InpSpikeJustJumpPct > 1.0 ||
      InpSpikeHistSignalMultiplier <= 0.0)
   {
      Print("ChartID<",ChartID(),">: [",__FILE__,"] [",__FUNCTION__,"] LINE ",__LINE__,
            " Invalid inputs. Check period/offset/sensitivity/KC/spike settings.");
      return(INIT_PARAMETERS_INCORRECT);
   }
   
   IndicatorSetString(INDICATOR_SHORTNAME,
                      StringFormat("TEMA_EMA_Trigger(%d,%d,OpenRef-%d)", InpTEMAPeriod, InpEMAPeriod, InpOpenRefOffset));
   SetIndexBuffer(0, BufferTEMA, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(1, BufferEMAofTEMA, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(2, BufferTriggerThreshold, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(3, BufferTemaEmaSpread, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(4, BufferDynBlackout, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(5, BufferArrowBuy, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(6, BufferArrowSell, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(7,  BufferTradePL, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(8,  BufferRunningPL, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(9,  BufferTradeCount, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(10, BufferWinPct, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(11, BufferShortCount, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(12, BufferShortWinPct, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(13, BufferLongCount, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(14, BufferLongWinPct, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(15, BufferKCWidthRatio, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(16, BufferKCUpper, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(17, BufferKCMiddle, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(18, BufferKCLower, INDICATOR_DATA);
   
   string readoutUnit = ReadoutUnitText();
   PlotIndexSetInteger(2, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetString(2, PLOT_LABEL, "Trigger Threshold ("+readoutUnit+")");
   PlotIndexSetDouble(2, PLOT_EMPTY_VALUE, EMPTY_VALUE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(3, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetString(3, PLOT_LABEL, "T/E Spread ("+readoutUnit+")");
   PlotIndexSetDouble(3, PLOT_EMPTY_VALUE, EMPTY_VALUE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(4, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetString(4, PLOT_LABEL, "Active Blackout Bars");
   PlotIndexSetDouble(4, PLOT_EMPTY_VALUE, EMPTY_VALUE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(5, PLOT_ARROW, 233);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(6, PLOT_ARROW, 234);
   PlotIndexSetDouble(5, PLOT_EMPTY_VALUE, EMPTY_VALUE);
   PlotIndexSetDouble(6, PLOT_EMPTY_VALUE, EMPTY_VALUE);

   //--- Trade simulation buffers in Data Window only
   PlotIndexSetInteger(7, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetString(7, PLOT_LABEL, "Trade P/L ("+readoutUnit+")");
   PlotIndexSetDouble(7, PLOT_EMPTY_VALUE, EMPTY_VALUE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(8, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetString(8, PLOT_LABEL, "Running P/L ("+readoutUnit+")");
   PlotIndexSetDouble(8, PLOT_EMPTY_VALUE, EMPTY_VALUE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(9, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetString(9, PLOT_LABEL, "Trade Count");
   PlotIndexSetDouble(9, PLOT_EMPTY_VALUE, EMPTY_VALUE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(10, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetString(10, PLOT_LABEL, "Win %");
   PlotIndexSetDouble(10, PLOT_EMPTY_VALUE, EMPTY_VALUE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(11, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetString(11, PLOT_LABEL, "Short Count");
   PlotIndexSetDouble(11, PLOT_EMPTY_VALUE, EMPTY_VALUE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(12, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetString(12, PLOT_LABEL, "Short Win %");
   PlotIndexSetDouble(12, PLOT_EMPTY_VALUE, EMPTY_VALUE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(13, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetString(13, PLOT_LABEL, "Long Count");
   PlotIndexSetDouble(13, PLOT_EMPTY_VALUE, EMPTY_VALUE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(14, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetString(14, PLOT_LABEL, "Long Win %");
   PlotIndexSetDouble(14, PLOT_EMPTY_VALUE, EMPTY_VALUE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(15, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetString(15, PLOT_LABEL, "KC Width Ratio");
   PlotIndexSetDouble(15, PLOT_EMPTY_VALUE, EMPTY_VALUE);
   PlotIndexSetDouble(16, PLOT_EMPTY_VALUE, EMPTY_VALUE);
   PlotIndexSetDouble(17, PLOT_EMPTY_VALUE, EMPTY_VALUE);
   PlotIndexSetDouble(18, PLOT_EMPTY_VALUE, EMPTY_VALUE);
   
   ArraySetAsSeries(BufferTEMA, false);
   ArraySetAsSeries(BufferEMAofTEMA, false);
   ArraySetAsSeries(BufferTemaEmaSpread, false);
   ArraySetAsSeries(BufferArrowBuy, false);
   ArraySetAsSeries(BufferArrowSell, false);
   ArraySetAsSeries(BufferTradePL, false);
   ArraySetAsSeries(BufferRunningPL, false);
   ArraySetAsSeries(BufferTradeCount, false);
   ArraySetAsSeries(BufferWinPct, false);
   ArraySetAsSeries(BufferShortCount, false);
   ArraySetAsSeries(BufferShortWinPct, false);
   ArraySetAsSeries(BufferLongCount, false);
   ArraySetAsSeries(BufferLongWinPct, false);
   ArraySetAsSeries(BufferDynBlackout, false);
   ArraySetAsSeries(BufferKCWidthRatio, false);
   ArraySetAsSeries(BufferTriggerThreshold, false);
   ArraySetAsSeries(BufferKCUpper, false);
   ArraySetAsSeries(BufferKCMiddle, false);
   ArraySetAsSeries(BufferKCLower, false);
   ArraySetAsSeries(BufferAggExecPrice, false);
   
   iHndlTEMA = iTEMA(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, InpTEMAPeriod, 0, InpTEMAApplied);
   if(iHndlTEMA == INVALID_HANDLE)
   {
      Print("ChartID<",ChartID(),">: [",__FILE__,"] [",__FUNCTION__,"] LINE ",__LINE__,
            " Failed to create TEMA handle. err=", GetLastError());
      return(INIT_FAILED);
   }
   
   iHndlEMAofTEMA = iMA(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, InpEMAPeriod, 0, MODE_EMA, (ENUM_APPLIED_PRICE)iHndlTEMA);
   if(iHndlEMAofTEMA == INVALID_HANDLE)
   {
      Print("ChartID<",ChartID(),">: [",__FILE__,"] [",__FUNCTION__,"] LINE ",__LINE__,
            " Failed to create EMA(TEMA) handle. err=", GetLastError());
      return(INIT_FAILED);
   }
   
   iHndlKC = iCustom(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, "DevoIndies\\Ind_KC_MJM",
                     InpKCPeriod, InpKCATRPeriod, InpKCMultiplier, InpKCApplied,
                     clrBlueViolet, 3, clrWhite, 2, clrBlueViolet, 3);
   if(iHndlKC == INVALID_HANDLE)
   {
      Print("ChartID<",ChartID(),">: [",__FILE__,"] [",__FUNCTION__,"] LINE ",__LINE__,
            " Failed to create KC handle. err=", GetLastError());
      return(INIT_FAILED);
   }
   
   g_arrowCfg.SetDefaults();
   g_labelCfg.SetDefaults();
   g_arrowCfg.longColor  = clrLime;
   g_arrowCfg.shortColor = clrRed;
   g_labelCfg.prefix = "T/E Trigger";
   g_maxPeriodsBack = MathMax(InpTEMAPeriod, InpEMAPeriod);
   g_maxPeriodsBack = MathMax(g_maxPeriodsBack, InpKCPeriod);
   g_maxPeriodsBack = MathMax(g_maxPeriodsBack, InpKCATRPeriod);
   g_maxPeriodsBack += (3 + InpOpenRefOffset);
   
   if(g_effectiveTradeStartDate == 0)
   {
      g_effectiveTradeStartDate = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 0);
      Print("");
      Print("ChartID<",ChartID(),">: [",__FILE__,"] [",__FUNCTION__,"] LINE ",__LINE__,
            " InpTradeStartDate=0; using current bar time<",
            TimeToString(g_effectiveTradeStartDate, TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES), ">");
   }
   
   if(InpTradeStopDate <= 0)
      g_tradeStopDT = D'3000.12.31 23:59';
   else
      g_tradeStopDT = InpTradeStopDate;
      
   if(InpEnableDiagPrints)
      Print("ChartID<",ChartID(),">: [",__FILE__,"] [",__FUNCTION__,"] LINE ",__LINE__," InpTradeStartDate<",InpTradeStartDate,
            "> InpTradeStopDate<",InpTradeStopDate,"> g_tradeStopDT<",g_tradeStopDT,">");
            
   return(INIT_SUCCEEDED);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OnDeinit(const int reason)
{
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, SIGNAL_PREFIX+"_");
   if(iHndlKC != INVALID_HANDLE)
      IndicatorRelease(iHndlKC);
   if(iHndlEMAofTEMA != INVALID_HANDLE)
      IndicatorRelease(iHndlEMAofTEMA);
   if(iHndlTEMA != INVALID_HANDLE)
      IndicatorRelease(iHndlTEMA);

   //--- Clean up trade simulation objects
   if(InpEnableTradeSim)
   {
      TS_Cleanup();
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Custom indicator iteration function                              |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int OnCalculate(const int rates_total,
                const int prev_calculated,
                const datetime &time[],
                const double &open[],
                const double &high[],
                const double &low[],
                const double &close[],
                const long &tick_volume[],
                const long &volume[],
                const int &spread[])
{
   if(rates_total <= g_maxPeriodsBack)
      return(0);

   int start = (prev_calculated == 0) ? g_maxPeriodsBack : prev_calculated - 1;
   if(start < 1)
      start = 1;

   if(prev_calculated == 0)
   {
      ResetAllStateAndBuffers();
      g_lastExportedBar = -1;
      InitCsvDebugFile();
   }

   int copyStart = MathMax(0, start - 3);
   int copyCount = rates_total - copyStart;

   double temaTail[];
   double emaTail[];
   double kcUpperTail[];
   double kcMiddleTail[];
   double kcLowerTail[];
   ArrayResize(temaTail, copyCount);
   ArrayResize(emaTail, copyCount);
   ArrayResize(kcUpperTail, copyCount);
   ArrayResize(kcMiddleTail, copyCount);
   ArrayResize(kcLowerTail, copyCount);
   ArraySetAsSeries(temaTail, false);
   ArraySetAsSeries(emaTail, false);
   ArraySetAsSeries(kcUpperTail, false);
   ArraySetAsSeries(kcMiddleTail, false);
   ArraySetAsSeries(kcLowerTail, false);

   int temaCopied = CopyBuffer(iHndlTEMA, 0, 0, copyCount, temaTail);
   int emaCopied  = CopyBuffer(iHndlEMAofTEMA, 0, 0, copyCount, emaTail);
   int kcUpperCopied  = CopyBuffer(iHndlKC, 0, 0, copyCount, kcUpperTail);
   int kcMiddleCopied = CopyBuffer(iHndlKC, 1, 0, copyCount, kcMiddleTail);
   int kcLowerCopied  = CopyBuffer(iHndlKC, 2, 0, copyCount, kcLowerTail);
   
   if(temaCopied != copyCount || emaCopied != copyCount ||
      kcUpperCopied != copyCount || kcMiddleCopied != copyCount || kcLowerCopied != copyCount)
   {
      Print("ChartID<",ChartID(),">: [",__FILE__,"] [",__FUNCTION__,"] LINE ",__LINE__,
            " CopyBuffer failed. temaCopied=",temaCopied,
            " emaCopied=",emaCopied,
            " kcUpperCopied=",kcUpperCopied,
            " kcMiddleCopied=",kcMiddleCopied,
            " kcLowerCopied=",kcLowerCopied,
            " expected=",copyCount,
            " err=",GetLastError());
      return(prev_calculated);
   }

   ArrayResize(BufferAggExecPrice, rates_total);

   double pointSize = MathPow(10.0, -_Digits);
   double eps = pointSize * 0.1;
   double pipSize = pointSize * 10.0;
   //--- Convert user inputs from readout units to raw price (once)
   double triggerSensInputPrice = ReadoutToPrice(InpTriggerSensitivity, pipSize);
   double flatSlopePrice   = ReadoutToPrice(InpFlatSlopePerBar, pipSize);
   double arrowOffsetPrice = ReadoutToPrice(InpObjectArrowOffset, pipSize);
   ENUM_TS_READOUT_MODE tsReadoutMode = TSReadoutMode();

   //--- Copy indicator values to buffers and calculate dynamic blackout
   for(int iKX = 0; iKX < copyCount; iKX++)
   {
      int iIX = copyStart + iKX;
      BufferTEMA[iIX] = temaTail[iKX];
      BufferEMAofTEMA[iIX] = emaTail[iKX];
      BufferKCUpper[iIX] = kcUpperTail[iKX];
      BufferKCMiddle[iIX] = kcMiddleTail[iKX];
      BufferKCLower[iIX] = kcLowerTail[iKX];
      BufferTemaEmaSpread[iIX] = PriceDeltaToReadout(BufferTEMA[iIX] - BufferEMAofTEMA[iIX]);
   
      int activeBlackout = InpSignalBlackout;
      if(InpUseDynamicBlackout && 
         BufferKCUpper[iIX] != EMPTY_VALUE && 
         BufferKCLower[iIX] != EMPTY_VALUE && 
         BufferKCMiddle[iIX] != EMPTY_VALUE)
      {
         double currWidth = MathAbs(BufferKCUpper[iIX] - BufferKCLower[iIX]);
         int lb = MathMin(InpKCWidthLookback, iIX + 1);
         double widthSum = 0.0;
         int widthCnt = 0;
         int from = iIX - lb + 1;
         if(from < 0) from = 0;
         for(int j = from; j <= iIX; j++)
         {
            if(BufferKCUpper[j] == EMPTY_VALUE || BufferKCLower[j] == EMPTY_VALUE)
               continue;
            widthSum += MathAbs(BufferKCUpper[j] - BufferKCLower[j]);
            widthCnt++;
         }
         double baselineWidth = (widthCnt > 0) ? (widthSum / widthCnt) : currWidth;
         double widthRatio = (baselineWidth > 0.0) ? (currWidth / baselineWidth) : 1.0;
         double slopePerBar = 0.0;
         if(iIX >= InpKCMidSlopePeriod && BufferKCMiddle[iIX - InpKCMidSlopePeriod] != EMPTY_VALUE)
            slopePerBar = (BufferKCMiddle[iIX] - BufferKCMiddle[iIX - InpKCMidSlopePeriod]) / InpKCMidSlopePeriod;
         
         bool isNarrow = (widthRatio < InpNarrowWidthRatio);
         bool isFlat = (MathAbs(slopePerBar) <= flatSlopePrice);
         if(isNarrow && isFlat)
         {
            double extra = (InpNarrowWidthRatio - widthRatio) * InpBlackoutScaleK;
            if(extra < 0.0) extra = 0.0;
            activeBlackout = InpSignalBlackout + (int)MathRound(extra);
            if(activeBlackout > InpBlackoutMax)
               activeBlackout = InpBlackoutMax;
         }
         BufferKCWidthRatio[iIX] = widthRatio;
      }
      BufferDynBlackout[iIX] = activeBlackout;
   }
   
   bool isNewBar = (rates_total != prev_calculated);
   bool isAggressive = IsAggressiveMode();
   int liveBar = rates_total - 1;
   
   if(isNewBar && g_provisionalSignal)
   {
      g_lastSignalBar = liveBar - 1;
      if(isAggressive && !g_aggrRecoveryActive && g_currentTrade.direction != 0)
         ArmAggressiveFlipCheck(g_lastSignalBar, g_currentTrade.direction, InpMockTradePriceSource);
      g_provisionalSignal = false;
      g_isCocked = false;
      g_cockedDir = 0;
      iDir = g_currentTrade.direction;
      g_crossBar = g_lastSignalBar;
   }

   //--- Handle spike that carried over from prior bar
   if(isNewBar && g_spikeActive && g_spikeBar < liveBar)
   {
      g_spikeFromPriorBar = true;
   }

   if(BufferTEMA[liveBar] != EMPTY_VALUE && BufferEMAofTEMA[liveBar] != EMPTY_VALUE)
      BufferTemaEmaSpread[liveBar] = PriceDeltaToReadout(BufferTEMA[liveBar] - BufferEMAofTEMA[liveBar]);

   if(InpEnableTradeSim)
   {
      double liveExitPrice = GetMockTradePrice(liveBar, close);
      UpdateOpenTradeLiveState(liveBar, liveExitPrice);
   }

   //--- Signals processing loop
   int loopEnd = rates_total;
   
   //--- Determine signal loop start: warmup bars before start date
   int signalStart = start;
   if(time[start] < g_effectiveTradeStartDate)
   {
      int warmup = 2 + InpOpenRefOffset;
      int startDateBar = -1;
      for(int i = start; i < rates_total; i++)
      {
         if(time[i] >= g_effectiveTradeStartDate)
         {
            startDateBar = i;
            break;
         }
      }
      signalStart = MathMax(start, startDateBar - warmup);
   }

   //+------------------------------------------------------------------+
   //| Signals process main loop                                        |
   //+------------------------------------------------------------------+
   for(int iIX = signalStart; iIX < loopEnd; iIX++)
   {
      //--- Stop processing if past stop date
      if(time[iIX] > g_tradeStopDT)
      {
         if(InpEnableDiagPrints)
            Print("ChartID<",ChartID(),">: [",__FILE__,"] [",__FUNCTION__,"] LINE ",__LINE__,
                  " DIAG STOP_DATE_BREAK iIX<",iIX,"> time[iIX]<",TimeToString(time[iIX],TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES),
                  "> g_tradeStopDT<",TimeToString(g_tradeStopDT,TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES),
                  "> signalStart<",signalStart,">");
         break;
      }
      
      if(prev_calculated == 0 || iIX >= prev_calculated)
      {
         BufferArrowBuy[iIX] = EMPTY_VALUE;
         BufferArrowSell[iIX] = EMPTY_VALUE;
      }
            
      int prevT2E = T2ESign(BufferTEMA[iIX - 1] - BufferEMAofTEMA[iIX - 1], eps);
      int currT2E = T2ESign(BufferTEMA[iIX] - BufferEMAofTEMA[iIX], eps);

      if(prevT2E == 0) prevT2E = iDir;
      if(currT2E == 0) currT2E = prevT2E;

      int prevT2KCM = 0;
      int currT2KCM = 0;
      int prevE2KCM = 0;
      int currE2KCM = 0;
      if(BufferTEMA[iIX - 1] != EMPTY_VALUE && BufferKCMiddle[iIX - 1] != EMPTY_VALUE)
         prevT2KCM = T2ESign(BufferTEMA[iIX - 1] - BufferKCMiddle[iIX - 1], eps);
      if(BufferTEMA[iIX] != EMPTY_VALUE && BufferKCMiddle[iIX] != EMPTY_VALUE)
         currT2KCM = T2ESign(BufferTEMA[iIX] - BufferKCMiddle[iIX], eps);
      if(BufferEMAofTEMA[iIX - 1] != EMPTY_VALUE && BufferKCMiddle[iIX - 1] != EMPTY_VALUE)
         prevE2KCM = T2ESign(BufferEMAofTEMA[iIX - 1] - BufferKCMiddle[iIX - 1], eps);
      if(BufferEMAofTEMA[iIX] != EMPTY_VALUE && BufferKCMiddle[iIX] != EMPTY_VALUE)
         currE2KCM = T2ESign(BufferEMAofTEMA[iIX] - BufferKCMiddle[iIX], eps);

      int prevT2KC = KCOuterState(BufferTEMA[iIX - 1], prevT2KCM, BufferKCUpper[iIX - 1], BufferKCLower[iIX - 1], eps);
      int currT2KC = KCOuterState(BufferTEMA[iIX], currT2KCM, BufferKCUpper[iIX], BufferKCLower[iIX], eps);
      int prevE2KC = KCOuterState(BufferEMAofTEMA[iIX - 1], prevE2KCM, BufferKCUpper[iIX - 1], BufferKCLower[iIX - 1], eps);
      int currE2KC = KCOuterState(BufferEMAofTEMA[iIX], currE2KCM, BufferKCUpper[iIX], BufferKCLower[iIX], eps);

      //=================================================================
      // SPIKE DETECTION AND PROCESSING (aggressive mode only)
      //=================================================================
      ENUM_SIGNAL_TYPE spikeSignal = SIGNAL_NONE;
      double spikeSignalPrice = 0.0;
      
      if(InpEnableSpikeDetection && isAggressive)
      {
         bool isLiveBar = (iIX == liveBar);
         
         if(isLiveBar)
         {
            //--- Live bar: intra-bar spike tracking
            
            //--- Check for spike reset condition first
            if(g_spikeActive)
            {
               CheckSpikeReset(iIX, close[iIX], BufferKCUpper[iIX], BufferKCLower[iIX]);
            }
            
            //--- Check for new spike condition (if not already tracking one)
            if(!g_spikeActive)
            {
               CheckSpikeCondition(iIX, high[iIX], low[iIX], close[iIX],
                                  BufferKCUpper[iIX], BufferKCLower[iIX]);
            }
            
            //--- Process active spike (intra-bar tracking)
            if(g_spikeActive)
            {
               spikeSignal = ProcessSpikeIntraBar(iIX, close[iIX], high[iIX], low[iIX], time[iIX]);
               if(spikeSignal != SIGNAL_NONE)
                  spikeSignalPrice = close[iIX];  // Use current price for live signals
            }
         }
         else
         {
            //--- Historical bar: use hindsight approximation
            double highNext = (iIX + 1 < rates_total) ? high[iIX + 1] : EMPTY_VALUE;
            double lowNext = (iIX + 1 < rates_total) ? low[iIX + 1] : EMPTY_VALUE;
            
            spikeSignal = CheckSpikeHistorical(iIX, high, low,
                                               BufferKCUpper[iIX], BufferKCLower[iIX],
//                                               highNext, lowNext,
                                               spikeSignalPrice);
         }
      }

      //=================================================================
      // EXISTING MA-BASED COCKING AND TRIGGER LOGIC
      //=================================================================
      
      // Cocking logic: only run on closed bars (not the nascent live bar)
      if(isNewBar && iIX < rates_total - 1)
      {
         if(iDir != 0)
         {
            bool candidateFound = false;
            bool cockedThisBar = false;
            for(int d = 1; d <= 2; d++)
            {
               int cand = iIX - d;
               if(cand <= g_crossBar || cand < 1 || cand + 1 >= rates_total)
                  continue;
               candidateFound = true;
               int refBar = MathMax(0, cand - InpOpenRefOffset);
               double refOpen = open[refBar];

               g_extremeBar = cand;
               g_extremeOpenRefBar = refBar;
               g_extremeOpen = refOpen;
               g_extremePrice = (iDir > 0) ? high[cand] : low[cand];
               if((iDir > 0 && close[iIX] < refOpen) || (iDir < 0 && close[iIX] > refOpen))
               {
                  g_isCocked = true;
                  g_cockedDir = (iDir > 0) ? -1 : 1;
                  g_cockLevel = refOpen;
                  cockedThisBar = true;

                  if(InpEnableDiagPrints && time[iIX] >= g_effectiveTradeStartDate && time[iIX] <= g_tradeStopDT)
                     Print("ChartID<",ChartID(),">: [",__FILE__,"] [",__FUNCTION__,"] LINE ",__LINE__,
                           " DIAG#3 COCKED iIX<",iIX,"> time<",TimeToString(time[iIX],TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES),"> iDir<",iDir,"> g_cockedDir<",g_cockedDir,
                           "> g_cockLevel<",g_cockLevel,"> close[iIX]<",close[iIX],
                           "> refOpen<",refOpen,"> cand<",cand,">");

                  break;
               }
            }
            g_pendingConfirm = (candidateFound && !cockedThisBar);
         }
      }

      bool rawCrossUp = (prevT2E < 0 && currT2E > 0);
      bool rawCrossDn = (prevT2E > 0 && currT2E < 0);
      bool rawCross = (rawCrossUp || rawCrossDn);

      bool revertedWas = g_revertedMode;
      bool revertedSetThisBar = false;
      if(isAggressive && rawCross)
      {
         int rawCrossDir = rawCrossUp ? 1 : -1;
         if(g_lastAggRawCrossDir != 0 && rawCrossDir != g_lastAggRawCrossDir)
         {
            g_revertedMode = true;
            revertedSetThisBar = true;
         }
         g_lastAggRawCrossDir = rawCrossDir;
      }

      double effectiveTriggerSensReadout = g_revertedMode ? 0.0 : InpTriggerSensitivity;
      double effectiveTriggerSensPrice = ReadoutToPrice(effectiveTriggerSensReadout, pipSize);
      double instantSpread = 0.0;
      if(BufferTEMA[iIX] != EMPTY_VALUE && BufferEMAofTEMA[iIX] != EMPTY_VALUE)
         instantSpread = MathAbs(BufferTEMA[iIX] - BufferEMAofTEMA[iIX]);
      bool qualifiedCross = (rawCross && (instantSpread >= effectiveTriggerSensPrice));

      if(InpEnableDiagPrints && time[iIX] >= g_effectiveTradeStartDate && time[iIX] <= g_tradeStopDT && rawCross)
         Print("ChartID<",ChartID(),">: [",__FILE__,"] [",__FUNCTION__,"] LINE ",__LINE__,
               " DIAG#4 CROSS iIX<",iIX,"> rawCrossUp<",rawCrossUp,"> rawCrossDn<",rawCrossDn,
               "> qualifiedCross<",qualifiedCross,"> revertedMode<",g_revertedMode,
               "> prevT2E<",prevT2E,"> currT2E<",currT2E,
               "> instantSpread<",instantSpread,"> effectiveSens<",effectiveTriggerSensPrice,
               "> g_isCocked<",g_isCocked,"> g_cockedDir<",g_cockedDir,">");
               
      if(InpEnableDiagPrints && time[iIX] >= g_effectiveTradeStartDate && time[iIX] <= g_tradeStopDT && revertedSetThisBar)
         Print("ChartID<",ChartID(),">: [",__FILE__,"] [",__FUNCTION__,"] LINE ",__LINE__,
               " DIAG#4A REVERTED_MODE_ON iIX<",iIX,"> revertedWas<",revertedWas,
               "> revertedNow<",g_revertedMode,"> rawCrossUp<",rawCrossUp,"> rawCrossDn<",rawCrossDn,
               "> lastAggRawCrossDir<",g_lastAggRawCrossDir,">");
               
      if(InpEnableDiagPrints && time[iIX] >= g_effectiveTradeStartDate && time[iIX] <= g_tradeStopDT && rawCross && !qualifiedCross)
         Print("ChartID<",ChartID(),">: [",__FILE__,"] [",__FUNCTION__,"] LINE ",__LINE__,
               " DIAG#4B CROSS_REJECTED iIX<",iIX,"> instantSpread<",instantSpread,
               "> requiredSens<",effectiveTriggerSensPrice,"> revertedMode<",g_revertedMode,">");

      BufferTriggerThreshold[iIX] = effectiveTriggerSensReadout;

      if(iDir == 0 && currT2E != 0 && isNewBar && iIX < rates_total - 1)
         ResetCycleForDirection(currT2E, iIX, open, high, low);

      ENUM_SIGNAL_TYPE signal = SIGNAL_NONE;
      ENUM_SIGNAL_TYPE signalCandidate = SIGNAL_NONE;
      bool forceCommittedSignal = false;
      bool isBackstopSignal = false;
      bool isSpikeTriggered = false;
      bool useAggressive = (isAggressive && !g_aggrRecoveryActive);
      bool conditionMet = false;
      
      if(InpEnableDiagPrints && time[iIX] >= g_effectiveTradeStartDate && time[iIX] <= g_tradeStopDT && rawCross)
         Print("ChartID<",ChartID(),">: [",__FILE__,"] [",__FUNCTION__,"] LINE ",__LINE__,
               " DIAG#4C MODE_SELECT iIX<",iIX,"> isAggressive<",isAggressive,
               "> g_aggrRecoveryActive<",g_aggrRecoveryActive,"> useAggressive<",useAggressive,
               "> g_triggerPending<",g_triggerPending,">");

      //=================================================================
      // SPIKE SIGNAL TAKES PRIORITY - merges into existing signal flow
      //=================================================================
      if(spikeSignal != SIGNAL_NONE)
      {
         signalCandidate = spikeSignal;
         isSpikeTriggered = true;
         forceCommittedSignal = true;  // Spike signals bypass provisional logic
         
         if(InpEnableDiagPrints && time[iIX] >= g_effectiveTradeStartDate && time[iIX] <= g_tradeStopDT)
            Print("ChartID<",ChartID(),">: [",__FILE__,"] [",__FUNCTION__,"] LINE ",__LINE__,
                  " SPIKE_SIGNAL_CANDIDATE iIX<",iIX,"> signal<",SignalToText(spikeSignal),
                  "> signalPrice<",spikeSignalPrice,">");
      }
      
      //=================================================================
      // EXISTING MA-BASED TRIGGERING LOGIC (only if no spike signal)
      //=================================================================
      if(signalCandidate == SIGNAL_NONE)
      {
         if(useAggressive)
         {
            if(g_isCocked)
            {
               double liveClose = close[iIX];
               double temaVal = BufferTEMA[iIX];
               double emaVal = BufferEMAofTEMA[iIX];

               if(InpMockTradePriceSource == MOCK_PRICE_AGG_TEMA)
               {
                  if(g_cockedDir == -1)
                     conditionMet = (liveClose < temaVal - effectiveTriggerSensPrice);
                  else
                     conditionMet = (liveClose > temaVal + effectiveTriggerSensPrice);
               }
               else if(InpMockTradePriceSource == MOCK_PRICE_AGG_EMA)
               {
                  if(g_cockedDir == -1)
                     conditionMet = (liveClose < emaVal - effectiveTriggerSensPrice);
                  else
                     conditionMet = (liveClose > emaVal + effectiveTriggerSensPrice);
               }
               else
               {
                  if(g_cockedDir == -1)
                     conditionMet = (liveClose < temaVal && liveClose < emaVal - effectiveTriggerSensPrice);
                  else
                     conditionMet = (liveClose > temaVal && liveClose > emaVal + effectiveTriggerSensPrice);
               }

               if(conditionMet)
                  signalCandidate = (g_cockedDir == 1) ? SIGNAL_LONG : SIGNAL_SHORT;
            }
         }
         else
         {
            if(rawCross)
            {
               if(g_isCocked)
               {
                  if(rawCrossDn && g_cockedDir == -1)
                  {
                     g_triggerPending = true;
                     g_triggerDir = -1;
                     g_triggerCrossBar = iIX;

                     if(InpEnableDiagPrints && time[iIX] >= g_effectiveTradeStartDate && time[iIX] <= g_tradeStopDT)
                        Print("ChartID<",ChartID(),">: [",__FILE__,"] [",__FUNCTION__,"] LINE ",__LINE__,
                              " DIAG#5 TRIGGER_PENDING iIX<",iIX,"> dir<SHORT> g_triggerCrossBar<",iIX,
                              "> g_isCocked<",g_isCocked,"> g_cockedDir<",g_cockedDir,">");
                  }
                  else if(rawCrossUp && g_cockedDir == 1)
                  {
                     g_triggerPending = true;
                     g_triggerDir = 1;
                     g_triggerCrossBar = iIX;

                     if(InpEnableDiagPrints && time[iIX] >= g_effectiveTradeStartDate && time[iIX] <= g_tradeStopDT)
                        Print("ChartID<",ChartID(),">: [",__FILE__,"] [",__FUNCTION__,"] LINE ",__LINE__,
                              " DIAG#5 TRIGGER_PENDING iIX<",iIX,"> dir<LONG> g_triggerCrossBar<",iIX,
                              "> g_isCocked<",g_isCocked,"> g_cockedDir<",g_cockedDir,">");
                  }
                  else
                  {
                     g_triggerPending = false;
                     g_triggerDir = 0;
                     g_triggerCrossBar = -1;
                  }
               }
               else
               {
                  g_triggerPending = false;
                  g_triggerDir = 0;
                  g_triggerCrossBar = -1;
               }
            }

            if(g_triggerPending)
            {
               bool wrongSide = ((g_triggerDir == 1 && currT2E < 0) || (g_triggerDir == -1 && currT2E > 0));
               if(wrongSide)
               {
                  if(InpEnableDiagPrints && time[iIX] >= g_effectiveTradeStartDate && time[iIX] <= g_tradeStopDT)
                     Print("ChartID<",ChartID(),">: [",__FILE__,"] [",__FUNCTION__,"] LINE ",__LINE__,
                           " DIAG#6A PENDING_CLEARED_WRONG_SIDE iIX<",iIX,"> g_triggerDir<",g_triggerDir,
                           "> currT2E<",currT2E,"> g_triggerCrossBar<",g_triggerCrossBar,">");
                  g_triggerPending = false;
                  g_triggerDir = 0;
                  g_triggerCrossBar = -1;
               }
               else
               {
                  double cumulativeSpread = 0.0;
                  int sumFrom = MathMax(0, g_triggerCrossBar);
                  if(sumFrom <= iIX)
                  {
                     for(int s = sumFrom; s <= iIX; s++)
                     {
                        if(BufferTEMA[s] == EMPTY_VALUE || BufferEMAofTEMA[s] == EMPTY_VALUE)
                           continue;
                        cumulativeSpread += MathAbs(BufferTEMA[s] - BufferEMAofTEMA[s]);
                     }
                  }

                  if(InpEnableDiagPrints && time[iIX] >= g_effectiveTradeStartDate && time[iIX] <= g_tradeStopDT)
                     Print("ChartID<",ChartID(),">: [",__FILE__,"] [",__FUNCTION__,"] LINE ",__LINE__,
                           " DIAG#6 SPREAD_CHECK iIX<",iIX,"> cumulativeSpread<",cumulativeSpread,
                           "> effectiveSens<",effectiveTriggerSensPrice,"> sumFrom<",sumFrom,
                           "> met<",(cumulativeSpread >= effectiveTriggerSensPrice),">");

                  if(cumulativeSpread >= effectiveTriggerSensPrice)
                  {
                     signalCandidate = (g_triggerDir == 1) ? SIGNAL_LONG : SIGNAL_SHORT;
                     g_triggerPending = false;
                     g_triggerDir = 0;
                     g_triggerCrossBar = -1;
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }

      //--- Common provisional management (mode-independent)
      if(g_provisionalSignal && signalCandidate == SIGNAL_NONE)
      {
         bool invalidated = false;
         if(useAggressive)
            invalidated = !conditionMet;
         else
            invalidated = ((g_currentTrade.direction == 1 && currT2E < 0) ||
                           (g_currentTrade.direction == -1 && currT2E > 0));
         if(invalidated)
            RevertProvisionalSignal(iIX);
      }

      //--- Cross-backstop guarantee (only if no spike and no other signal)
      if(rawCross && signalCandidate == SIGNAL_NONE)
      {
         isBackstopSignal = true;
         signalCandidate = rawCrossUp ? SIGNAL_LONG : SIGNAL_SHORT;
      }

      double effectiveBlackout = (BufferDynBlackout[iIX] == EMPTY_VALUE) ? InpSignalBlackout : BufferDynBlackout[iIX];
      if(isAggressive)
      {
         effectiveBlackout = effectiveBlackout - 1.0;
         if(effectiveBlackout < 0.0)
            effectiveBlackout = 0.0;
      }
      if(g_revertedMode)
         effectiveBlackout = 0.0;
         
      //--- Spike signals bypass blackout entirely
      if(isSpikeTriggered)
         effectiveBlackout = 0.0;
         
      bool canFireNow = ((iIX - g_lastSignalBar) >= effectiveBlackout && time[iIX] >= g_effectiveTradeStartDate && time[iIX] <= g_tradeStopDT);
      if(isBackstopSignal || isSpikeTriggered)
         canFireNow = (time[iIX] >= g_effectiveTradeStartDate && time[iIX] <= g_tradeStopDT);

      if(InpEnableDiagPrints && time[iIX] >= g_effectiveTradeStartDate && time[iIX] <= g_tradeStopDT && signalCandidate != SIGNAL_NONE)
         Print("ChartID<",ChartID(),">: [",__FILE__,"] [",__FUNCTION__,"] LINE ",__LINE__,
               " DIAG#7 CANDIDATE iIX<",iIX,"> time<",TimeToString(time[iIX],TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES),"> signalCandidate<",SignalToText(signalCandidate),
               "> canFireNow<",canFireNow,"> g_lastSignalBar<",g_lastSignalBar,
               "> BufferDynBlackout[iIX]<",BufferDynBlackout[iIX],"> barGap<",(iIX - g_lastSignalBar),
               "> effectiveBlackout<",effectiveBlackout,"> revertedMode<",g_revertedMode,
               "> effectiveSens<",effectiveTriggerSensPrice,"> isSpikeTriggered<",isSpikeTriggered,">");

      if(signalCandidate != SIGNAL_NONE)
      {
         if(canFireNow)
         {
            signal = signalCandidate;
            g_blackoutSignalPending = false;
            g_blackoutSignalDir = 0;
            g_blackoutSignalBar = -1;
            if(isBackstopSignal)
            {
               g_triggerPending = false;
               g_triggerDir = 0;
               g_triggerCrossBar = -1;
            }
         }
         else if(!isBackstopSignal && !isSpikeTriggered)
         {
            g_blackoutSignalPending = true;
            g_blackoutSignalDir = (signalCandidate == SIGNAL_LONG) ? 1 : -1;
            g_blackoutSignalBar = iIX;
         }
      }

      if(signal == SIGNAL_NONE && g_blackoutSignalPending)
      {
         bool retraced = ((g_blackoutSignalDir == 1 && currT2E < 0) || (g_blackoutSignalDir == -1 && currT2E > 0));
         if(retraced)
         {
            g_blackoutSignalPending = false;
            g_blackoutSignalDir = 0;
            g_blackoutSignalBar = -1;
         }
         else if(canFireNow)
         {
            signal = (g_blackoutSignalDir == 1) ? SIGNAL_LONG : SIGNAL_SHORT;
            g_blackoutSignalPending = false;
            g_blackoutSignalDir = 0;
            g_blackoutSignalBar = -1;
         }
      }

      if(!g_aggrRecoveryActive && g_aggrFlipCheckPending && iIX >= g_aggrFlipCheckBar)
      {
         g_aggrFlipCheckPending = false;
         if(IsWrongSideAggressiveTarget(iIX, close, effectiveTriggerSensPrice, g_aggrFinalDir, g_aggrFinalMode))
         {
            signal = (g_aggrFinalDir > 0) ? SIGNAL_SHORT : SIGNAL_LONG;
            forceCommittedSignal = true;
            g_aggrFlipCheckPending = false;
            g_aggrFlipCheckBar = -1;
            g_blackoutSignalPending = false;
            g_blackoutSignalDir = 0;
            g_blackoutSignalBar = -1;
            g_triggerPending = false;
            g_triggerDir = 0;
            g_triggerCrossBar = -1;
         }
      }

      //--- Global guard: never fire duplicate-direction signals in a row
      if(signal != SIGNAL_NONE && g_currentTrade.direction != 0)
      {
         int signalDir = (signal == SIGNAL_LONG) ? 1 : -1;
         if(signalDir == g_currentTrade.direction)
         {
            signal = SIGNAL_NONE;
            forceCommittedSignal = false;
         }
      }

      if(signal != SIGNAL_NONE)
      {
         if(forceCommittedSignal && !isSpikeTriggered)
            g_aggrRecoveryActive = true;

         if(InpEnableDiagPrints && time[iIX] >= g_effectiveTradeStartDate && time[iIX] <= g_tradeStopDT)
            Print("ChartID<",ChartID(),">: [",__FILE__,"] [",__FUNCTION__,"] LINE ",__LINE__,
                  " DIAG#8 SIGNAL_FIRED iIX<",iIX,"> time<",TimeToString(time[iIX],TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES),"> signal<",SignalToText(signal),
                  "> useAggressive<",useAggressive,"> revertedMode<",g_revertedMode,
                  "> effectiveSens<",effectiveTriggerSensPrice,"> effectiveBlackout<",effectiveBlackout,
                  "> isSpikeTriggered<",isSpikeTriggered,">");

         if(g_signalStartDT == 0)
            g_signalStartDT = time[iIX];

         if(InpEnableTradeSim)
         {
            bool isProvisional = (!forceCommittedSignal && !isNewBar && !isBackstopSignal && !isSpikeTriggered);
            if(isProvisional)
            {
               g_snapshotStats = g_tradeStats;
               g_snapshotTrade = g_currentTrade;
            }

            //--- Determine execution price
            double execPrice = close[iIX];
            
            if(isSpikeTriggered && spikeSignalPrice > 0)
            {
               //--- Use spike-calculated signal price
               execPrice = spikeSignalPrice;
            }
            else if(InpMockTradePriceSource == MOCK_PRICE_AGG_TEMA || InpMockTradePriceSource == MOCK_PRICE_AGG_EMA)
            {
               if(isProvisional || isBackstopSignal)
               {
                  execPrice = close[iIX];
               }
               else
               {
                  double basePrice = (InpMockTradePriceSource == MOCK_PRICE_AGG_TEMA) ? BufferTEMA[iIX] : BufferEMAofTEMA[iIX];
                  if(basePrice == EMPTY_VALUE)
                     execPrice = close[iIX];
                  else
                     execPrice = basePrice + ((signal == SIGNAL_LONG) ? effectiveTriggerSensPrice : -effectiveTriggerSensPrice);
               }
            }
            else if(InpMockTradePriceSource == MOCK_PRICE_TEMA)
            {
               execPrice = (BufferTEMA[iIX] != EMPTY_VALUE) ? BufferTEMA[iIX] : close[iIX];
            }
            else if(InpMockTradePriceSource == MOCK_PRICE_EMA_TEMA)
            {
               execPrice = (BufferEMAofTEMA[iIX] != EMPTY_VALUE) ? BufferEMAofTEMA[iIX] : close[iIX];
            }
            
            BufferAggExecPrice[iIX] = execPrice;
            BufferAggExecPrice[iIX] = execPrice;
            
            color savedLong = g_arrowCfg.longColor;
            color savedShort = g_arrowCfg.shortColor;
            if(isSpikeTriggered)
            {
               g_arrowCfg.longColor = SPIKE_LONG_COLOR;
               g_arrowCfg.shortColor = clrYellow;
            }
            HandleSignalEvent(iIX, signal,
                              true, true, true, true,
                              InpShowArrows,
                              time,
                              BufferAggExecPrice,
                              g_signalStartDT,
                              g_currentTrade,
                              g_tradeStats,
                              g_arrowCfg,
                              g_labelCfg,
                              (InpSoundTradeAlarm && time[iIX] >= g_effectiveTradeStartDate && time[iIX] <= g_tradeStopDT),
                              BufferTradePL,
                              BufferRunningPL,
                              BufferTradeCount,
                              BufferWinPct,
                              BufferShortCount,
                              BufferShortWinPct,
                              BufferLongCount,
                              BufferLongWinPct,
                              BufferArrowBuy,
                              BufferArrowSell,
                              tsReadoutMode);
            if(isSpikeTriggered)
            {
               g_arrowCfg.longColor = savedLong;
               g_arrowCfg.shortColor = savedShort;
            }
         }
         
         //--- Reposition object arrows
         if(InpShowArrows && g_currentTrade.arrowName != "" && ObjectFind(0, g_currentTrade.arrowName) >= 0 &&
            BufferKCUpper[iIX] != EMPTY_VALUE && BufferKCMiddle[iIX] != EMPTY_VALUE &&
            BufferKCLower[iIX] != EMPTY_VALUE && BufferTEMA[iIX] != EMPTY_VALUE)
         {
            double mid = BufferKCMiddle[iIX];
            double arrowPrice = 0.0;

            if(signal == SIGNAL_SHORT)
            {
               double baseUpper = BufferKCUpper[iIX];
               double distUpper = MathAbs(baseUpper - mid);
               double distTema  = MathAbs(BufferTEMA[iIX] - mid);
               double base = (distTema > distUpper) ? BufferTEMA[iIX] : baseUpper;
               arrowPrice = base + arrowOffsetPrice;
            }
            else
            {
               double baseLower = BufferKCLower[iIX];
               double distLower = MathAbs(baseLower - mid);
               double distTema  = MathAbs(BufferTEMA[iIX] - mid);
               double base = (distTema > distLower) ? BufferTEMA[iIX] : baseLower;
               arrowPrice = base - arrowOffsetPrice;
            }
            ObjectMove(0, g_currentTrade.arrowName, 0, time[iIX], arrowPrice);
         }

         if(!forceCommittedSignal && !isNewBar && !isBackstopSignal && !isSpikeTriggered)
         {
            g_provisionalSignal = true;
         }
         else
         {
            g_lastSignalBar = iIX;
            g_provisionalSignal = false;
            g_isCocked = false;
            g_cockedDir = 0;
            g_cockLevel = 0.0;
            iDir = (signal == SIGNAL_LONG) ? 1 : -1;
            g_crossBar = iIX;
            if(useAggressive && !forceCommittedSignal && !g_aggrRecoveryActive && g_currentTrade.direction != 0)
               ArmAggressiveFlipCheck(iIX, g_currentTrade.direction, InpMockTradePriceSource);
            if(forceCommittedSignal && !isSpikeTriggered)
               g_aggrRecoveryActive = false;
         }
      }

      //--- Uncock only after trigger decisions on this bar (closed bars only)
      if(g_isCocked && isNewBar && iIX < rates_total - 1)
      {
         if(g_cockedDir == -1 && close[iIX] > g_cockLevel)
         {
            if(InpEnableDiagPrints && time[iIX] >= g_effectiveTradeStartDate && time[iIX] <= g_tradeStopDT)
               Print("ChartID<",ChartID(),">: [",__FILE__,"] [",__FUNCTION__,"] LINE ",__LINE__,
                     " DIAG#9 UNCOCK iIX<",iIX,"> g_cockedDir<",g_cockedDir,
                     "> close[iIX]<",close[iIX],"> g_cockLevel<",g_cockLevel,">");
            if(g_provisionalSignal)
               RevertProvisionalSignal(iIX);
         }
         else if(g_cockedDir == 1 && close[iIX] < g_cockLevel)
         {
            if(InpEnableDiagPrints && time[iIX] >= g_effectiveTradeStartDate && time[iIX] <= g_tradeStopDT)
               Print("ChartID<",ChartID(),">: [",__FILE__,"] [",__FUNCTION__,"] LINE ",__LINE__,
                     " DIAG#9 UNCOCK iIX<",iIX,"> g_cockedDir<",g_cockedDir,
                     "> close[iIX]<",close[iIX],"> g_cockLevel<",g_cockLevel,">");
            if(g_provisionalSignal)
               RevertProvisionalSignal(iIX);
         }
      }

      if(iIX > g_lastExportedBar)
      {
         double currWidth = MathAbs(BufferKCUpper[iIX] - BufferKCLower[iIX]);
         double csvWidth = (currWidth != EMPTY_VALUE) ? PriceDeltaToReadout(currWidth) : EMPTY_VALUE;
         double slopePerBar = 0.0;
         if(iIX >= InpKCMidSlopePeriod && BufferKCMiddle[iIX - InpKCMidSlopePeriod] != EMPTY_VALUE)
            slopePerBar = (BufferKCMiddle[iIX] - BufferKCMiddle[iIX - InpKCMidSlopePeriod]) / InpKCMidSlopePeriod;

         double csvSlope = (slopePerBar != EMPTY_VALUE) ? PriceDeltaToReadout(slopePerBar) : EMPTY_VALUE;
         AppendCsvDebugRow(time[iIX], 
                           iIX,
                           open[iIX], 
                           high[iIX], 
                           low[iIX], 
                           close[iIX],
                           BufferTEMA[iIX], 
                           BufferEMAofTEMA[iIX],
                           BufferKCUpper[iIX], 
                           BufferKCMiddle[iIX], 
                           BufferKCLower[iIX],
                           csvWidth, 
                           BufferKCWidthRatio[iIX], 
                           csvSlope,
                           BufferDynBlackout[iIX],
                           rawCrossUp, 
                           rawCrossDn, 
                           rawCross,
                           signal);
         g_lastExportedBar = iIX;
      }

      //--- Cross after signal check: trigger consumes prior cocked state, then reset cycle (closed bars only)
      if(qualifiedCross && currT2E != 0 && isNewBar && iIX < rates_total - 1)
      {
         if(InpEnableDiagPrints && time[iIX] >= g_effectiveTradeStartDate && time[iIX] <= g_tradeStopDT)
            Print("ChartID<",ChartID(),">: [",__FILE__,"] [",__FUNCTION__,"] LINE ",__LINE__,
                  " DIAG#10 RESET_CYCLE iIX<",iIX,"> currT2E<",currT2E,
                  "> rawCrossUp<",rawCrossUp,"> rawCrossDn<",rawCrossDn,
                  "> qualifiedCross<",qualifiedCross,"> revertedMode<",g_revertedMode,
                  "> g_triggerPending<",g_triggerPending,">");

         if(g_provisionalSignal)
            RevertProvisionalSignal(iIX);
         ResetCycleForDirection(currT2E, iIX, open, high, low, g_triggerPending);
         g_aggrRecoveryActive = false;
         g_aggrFlipCheckPending = false;
         g_aggrFlipCheckBar = -1;
         g_aggrFinalDir = 0;
         g_aggrFinalMode = MOCK_PRICE_AGG_EMA;
         if(InpEnableDiagPrints && time[iIX] >= g_effectiveTradeStartDate && time[iIX] <= g_tradeStopDT && g_revertedMode)
            Print("ChartID<",ChartID(),">: [",__FILE__,"] [",__FUNCTION__,"] LINE ",__LINE__,
                  " DIAG#10A REVERTED_MODE_OFF iIX<",iIX,"> qualifiedCross<",qualifiedCross,">");
         g_revertedMode = false;
         continue;
      }

   } // end signal processing loop

   return(rates_total);
}
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+

Claude2026-02-25 03:01 UTC

The flip check is the culprit. After the spike resolves, a subsequent backstop or normal signal arms ArmAggressiveFlipCheck. When the flip check fires, it sets forceCommittedSignal = true but isSpikeTriggered = false and isBackstopSignal = false.

In the pricing chain:

if(isSpikeTriggered && spikeSignalPrice > 0)
   execPrice = spikeSignalPrice;           // no — not spike
else if(AGG mode)
{
   if(isProvisional || isBackstopSignal)
      execPrice = close[iIX];              // no — neither
   else
      execPrice = basePrice + offset;      // YES — synthetic EMA ± sensitivity
}

It lands in the synthetic branch: EMA value ± trigger sensitivity. Price is $5171 but EMA is at $5181. Signal gets priced at ~$5181 — outside the bar, completely fictional.

The fix: forceCommittedSignal signals from the flip check should also use close[iIX]. The condition should be:

if(isProvisional || isBackstopSignal || forceCommittedSignal)
   execPrice = close[iIX];

This covers flip check recovery AND any other forced-commit scenario. Normal aggressive triggers (which are never forceCommittedSignal) still get synthetic pricing as intended.
